11	## 0. TL;DR
12	A coach or parent in Bangalore should land on KhelSutra, feel at home, set up their camera with confidence, and get highlights — **in Kannada or Hindi**, with **zero hand-holding**. This project isolates that goal into one tracked workstream-pair: **(A)** make the UI super friendly for non-tech-savvy users, and **(B)** localize to Hindi + Kannada. Both surfaces (`web/` React SPA + `site/` vanilla marketing) have **zero i18n today** `[verified]`, so the foundation — shared `locales/{en,kn,hi}` catalogs + a negotiation contract + CI parity — is the real deliverable; translations follow. **The single most important caveat:** translations are **human-reviewed** (Gemini drafts are *inference only, never training* — the standing guardrail), and Kannada/Hindi rendering is **blocked on the OFL font decision (D-FONT)**. The cheapest unblocked high-impact first slice is the **SPA i18n scaffold + language switcher (en-only)** — it locks the shared catalog/negotiation contract and adds the render-test safety net the SPA currently lacks.
13	
14	## 1. Problem & goal
15	**Why now.** The public site (khelsutra.guru) is live, clean, honest, and tested. The PMF beachhead is **Bangalore badminton academies**; many coaches, parents, and players are far more comfortable in **Kannada** than English, and most are **not highly tech-savvy** `[verified — design intent]` (`UI_PROPOSAL.md:25,28`, `I18N_PLAN.md:10-13`, `PMF_MARKET_RESEARCH.md:116`, `KHELSUTRA_PRODUCT_OVERVIEW.md:13/50/61`). An English-only, knob-heavy product adds friction at the exact point of first contact.
16	
17	**Concrete outcome ("good" looks like):** a Kannada/Hindi-comfortable, phone-first, non-technical coach/parent can — without help — (1) understand what the product does and how to record reliably, (2) request access / use the console, (3) pick rallies and get a reel, and (4) share it on WhatsApp, **entirely in their language**.

36	## 3. Foundation — research / prior art
37	**Current-state facts `[verified]` this session** (adversarially checked against the working tree):
38	- **`web/` SPA: zero i18n.** `web/package.json` deps are only `react`/`react-dom`; no `i18next`/`react-intl`/`formatjs`; no `locales/` dir; no `t()`/`useTranslation`/`data-i18n` anywhere. User-facing English is hardcoded in `.tsx` (e.g. `QueryBar.tsx:46-47,56,72,76,78`; `App.tsx:115,211,224`) — ~50 literals + ~16 derived labels.
39	- **`site/` marketing: vanilla, zero i18n.** Three `<html lang="en">` files (`public/{index,capture,own-model}.html`) with inline CSS, no framework; the only scripts are a CF analytics beacon (`index.html:595`) + one inline form/reel IIFE (`index.html:597-633`). `server.js` hardcodes English responses; no locale handling.
40	- **Recording-education is *not* greenfield.** The homepage "Record it right" gist (`index.html:324-364`) + the `/capture` checklist (`capture.html`, routed `server.js:51`, guarded `assets.test.js:94`) already ship the user-facing best-practices surface. *Unshipped:* the empirical-threshold framing and the programmatic pre-flight validator (a code change, not a page).
41	- **Persona is design intent, not built.** kn/hi + zero-knobs are **proposed/owner-gated**; the app is English-only today (`KHELSUTRA_PRODUCT_OVERVIEW.md:61`). It's Kannada-**first**, Hindi-**next** — not Kannada-only.
42	
43	**Localization prior art `[researched]`:**
44	- **CLDR plural categories:** Hindi = `{one, other}`; Kannada = `{one, other}`. Both are 2-form (same shape as English) → once ICU MessageFormat is in the catalog, adding `hi`/`kn` plurals is **catalog-only**, no code. (ICU still required because English `count>1?'s':''` hand-rolling — present today in the SPA — doesn't generalize.)
45	- **react-i18next** (web/): `i18next` + `react-i18next` + `i18next-browser-languagedetector` + an ICU/plural post-processor; lazy-load per-locale JSON; persist via `localStorage`.
46	- **Shared catalog + tiny shim** (site/): the SAME i18next-flavored JSON consumed by a ~30-line `t(key, vars)` shim that walks `data-i18n` attributes — no build step, no framework, mirrors i18next interpolation/plural rules for the 2-form languages.
47	- **Noto Sans Kannada / Noto Sans Devanagari** are **OFL-1.1**; OFL permits commercial bundling/redistribution (fonts = media, not code/weights). Self-host (e.g. `@fontsource`) with `font-display: swap` + subsetting. **Gated on D-FONT.**
48	- **Non-tech-savvy / HCI4D patterns:** icon **+ text** (never icon-only), non-color selection indicators, large touch targets, mobile-first, WhatsApp as the share channel, a prominent persistent language switcher.
49	
50	## 4. Design / architecture
51	**Two surfaces, one shared source of truth.**
52	
53	```
54	locales/                      # shared catalogs — co-located in khelsutra (D-CATALOG-HOME, see §8)
55	  en/  common.json            # source of truth — devs author keys here
56	  kn/  common.json            # Kannada  (Gemini-draft → human-review)
57	  hi/  common.json            # Hindi    (next — the extensibility proof)
58	
59	web/   → react-i18next        # I18N_PLAN P3: provider in main.tsx; useTranslation in components
60	site/  → t(key,vars) shim      # I18N_PLAN P1 retargeted: data-i18n attrs over the static HTML
61	```
62	
63	- **Negotiation contract (one order, both surfaces):** `persisted pref (localStorage) → switcher / ?lang=<code> → navigator/Accept-Language → "en"`. Supported locales in a single module; anything else falls back to `en`; **a missing key degrades to English, never to a raw key.**
64	- **Keys, never prose** — `t("compile.button")`, with ICU for interpolation/plurals: `"{count, plural, one {# rally} other {# rallies}}"`. This replaces the SPA's current hand-rolled `Segment${count>1?'s':''}`.
65	- **Translation pipeline (guardrail-clean):** dev authors `en` keys → `scripts/i18n_draft.py`-style Gemini **draft** (inference only, never training; flags every value for review) → **owner/native-speaker review** → CI key-parity → merge. Gemini never touches `en`, never invents keys.
66	- **CI enforcement (keeps it "by design"):** (1) **key-parity** — every locale has exactly the `en` key set (drop a stub `ta.json` → CI lists every gap = the extensibility proof); (2) **no-bare-strings** — flags user-facing literals not routed through `t()` (curated allowlist to start).
67	- **Fonts:** bundle Noto Sans Kannada + Devanagari **iff D-FONT = OFL carve-out**; this also unblocks a Kannada/Hindi **reel watermark** (engine `WatermarkConfig.font_path` already takes a path — config, not code).
68	
69	**Non-tech-savvy UX levers** (ranked impact × effort, grounded in personas; surface-tagged):
70	
71	| Lever | Surface | Impact | Effort | Note |
72	|---|---|---|---|---|
73	| Plain-language copy — strip engine jargon, keep honest caveats | both | high | small | banners + footer carry backend vocabulary today |
74	| Localized **WhatsApp share** for the downloaded reel | both | high | small | WhatsApp is *the* channel for this audience |
75	| **Language switcher** persisted via a shared localStorage key | both | high | small | site promises kn/hi but ships no switcher |
76	| Localize **recording-education** (capture + gist) kn→hi | site | high | medium | gates input quality; the highest-leverage kn content |
77	| Mobile-first touch targets; tooltip-text → visible | web | high | medium | tiny targets + desktop layout + invisible tooltips on phones |
78	| Icon **+ text** + non-color selection indicator | web | medium | small | never icon-only |
79	| Friendly engine-error mapping over raw FastAPI `detail` | web | medium | medium | `CompileResult` passes engine text untranslated |
80	| Plain-language framing for the Request-Access GPU/storage Qs | site | medium | small | "GPU"/"S3/GCS" are intimidating |
81	| Background-processing reassurance + status as icon chips | web | medium | small | honest expectations on slow uplinks |
82	| Fix English-source recording gaps (a diagram) before translating | site | medium | medium | the diagram is reusable across all 3 languages |
83	| Tap-only filter controls alongside the English query bar | web | medium | large | the `parseQuery()` parser is English-grammar only |
84	| Keep the honest *today-vs-roadmap* split through translation | both | medium | small | never soften "experimental"/"not available yet" |
85	
86	**Reuse map (what's new vs reused):** *Reused* — the i18next-flavored JSON + negotiation contract + ICU plurals + key-parity/no-bare-strings CI + draft→review pipeline (all from `I18N_PLAN.md`); the shipped recording pages; the SPA's `lib/format.ts`/`lib/query.ts`; `site/src/assets.test.js` + the SPA's vitest harness. *New* — `locales/` catalogs; the SPA react-i18next provider + switcher; the site `t()` shim + `data-i18n` wiring; Noto font bundle (gated); a jsdom render-test env for the SPA.
87	
88	## 5. Privacy / scope note
89	i18n touches **presentation only** — no new personal-data collection, no biometrics, minors-excluded posture unchanged. WhatsApp share opens the user's own client with a pre-filled message; it sends nothing server-side. Gemini-draft translation is inference, not training (no user data in the loop).
90	
91	## 6. Honest limits — what this does NOT do
92	- A green CI / DONE status proves **key-parity + render + no-bare-strings**, **not** translation *quality* — that's human-gated and owner-paced.
93	- Kannada/Hindi will **not render** until the Noto fonts land (D-FONT); until then kn/hi catalogs exist but display tofu/fallback.
94	- The SPA is **job-tethered**; localizing **dynamic engine errors/report text** needs engine P2 (owner-gated, cross-repo, D-BACKEND-DEPTH) — out of this DoD.
95	- The query bar stays **English-grammar parsing**; kn/hi users rely on tap-only controls (a later, larger slice), not free-text Kannada queries.
96	- **Mobile-*perfect*** layout is an alpha non-goal (usable-on-phone is in scope; pixel-perfect is not).
97	
98	## 6.5 Testing strategy — automatic language negotiation (rigorous, owner-required)
99	**The bar (owner, #41):** a user whose **computer/browser language is Kannada or Hindi must get the respective version automatically** — that is the core promise and it is **proven by tests at every layer, never assumed.** Auto-selection IS the negotiation order (persisted pref → `?lang=` → browser/`Accept-Language` → `en`); each step gets a test.
100	
101	**Negotiation matrix — every row is an automated test:**
102	
103	| # | Signal under test | Setup | Expected | Proven in |
104	|---|---|---|---|---|
105	| N1 | **Browser/OS = Kannada** | no persisted pref, no `?lang`, `navigator.languages=["kn-IN", …]` | resolves to **kn** → Kannada content (once the kn catalog ships) | **B1** detection · **B5** content |
106	| N2 | **Browser/OS = Hindi** | `navigator.languages=["hi-IN", …]` | resolves to **hi** → Hindi content | **B6** (hi added to the supported set + catalog) |
107	| N3 | **`?lang=` override** | `?lang=kn`, browser = en | kn | B1 |
108	| N4 | **Persisted pref wins** | `localStorage=kn`, browser = en, no `?lang` | kn (a returning user keeps their choice) | B1 |
109	| N5 | **Region subtag normalizes** | `navigator.language="kn-IN"` | base **kn** (not a "kn-IN" miss) | B1 |
110	| N6 | **Unsupported language** | `navigator="ta-IN"` (Tamil, not yet supported) | **en** fallback — never a locale we can't serve | B1 |
111	| N7 | **Missing key in active locale** | kn active, a key absent from `kn/common.json` | **en** fallback for that key (no raw key shown) | B1/B5 |
112	| N8 | **Static site (vanilla shim)** | `Accept-Language: kn` / `?lang=kn` on a `site/` page | kn-rendered page (same order as the SPA) | B3 |
113	| N9 | **Backend (engine P2)** | `Accept-Language: kn` to the API | kn customer/report text | B7 (owner-gated) |
114	
115	**Levels & tooling:**
116	- **SPA (`web/`):** jsdom tests stub `navigator.language`/`navigator.languages`, `localStorage`, and the `?lang` query → assert `i18n.language`/`resolvedLanguage` + rendered text per the matrix. *(B1 lands N1/N3–N7 at the **detection** level now — `kn` is supported; the kn/hi **content** assertions ride the catalogs in B5/B6.)*
117	- **Static site (B3):** the shared `t()` shim's negotiation is unit-tested the same way; plus a server test that `Accept-Language: kn`/`?lang=kn` returns the kn-rendered page.
118	- **CI key-parity:** guarantees every locale has every key, so a correctly-negotiated locale can never fall through to a raw key.
119	- **End-to-end (manual, owner machine — per I18N_PLAN §6):** set the real OS/browser to Kannada, then Hindi → load both surfaces → confirm the right language renders **and the Noto fonts display** (the cloud CI container can't render fonts; the owner eyeball is the final gate).
120	- **⚠ Query parsing / semantic understanding (forward-looking, owner directive 2026-06-21) — i18n testing is MANDATORY there.** The query bar is **English-grammar-only** today (a documented limit, tracker A9): the user types English queries (`over 10s`, `top 15 longest`) and a localized **sample-query prompt** shows them what to type in *every* locale (kn/hi included). The **moment** the parser gains **semantic understanding** or accepts **Kannada/Hindi query input** (e.g. typing a Kannada/Hindi phrase, or a conversational/NL builder — I18N_PLAN P4), i18n tests become a **merge gate**: a kn/hi query (typed or spoken) must parse to the **correct selection**, the localized **parse-feedback** ("parsed-pill") must render in the active locale, and the unsupported/empty-result messages must localize. **No query-NL / semantic-parsing work merges without those tests.**
121	
122	A locale that's reachable by the switcher but **not auto-selected from the browser language is a bug, not a "later".** This matrix is a DoD gate (§9).
123

154	4. **D-SURFACE-FIRST — ✅ ADOPTED → lead with the SPA scaffold (B1), localize site recording-education early, SKIP the dead static-UI retrofit.**
155	5. **D-CATALOG-HOME — ✅ ADOPTED & IMPLEMENTED (B3) → repo-root `/locales/{en,kn,hi,es,da,id}/common.json` is the single source of truth.** The SPA imports it directly (re-pointed to `../../../locales`; Vite resolves the workspace root via the repo `.git` marker, so dev/build/test all work). The dependency-free site consumes it through one tiny build step — `npm run copy:locales` mirrors `/locales` → `site/public/locales/` (auto-runs `predeploy`), with a **drift-guard test** (`site/src/locales-drift.test.js`) failing CI if the shipped copy goes stale. (The engine `I18N_PLAN` assumed the indexer repo; this is the adaptation.)

79	<script type="module" src="/i18n.js"></script>
80	<div class="sheet">
81	  <div class="head">
82	    <div class="top">
83	      <a class="brand" href="/">Khel<span class="play" aria-hidden="true"></span>Sutra</a>
84	      <div class="nav-right">
85	        <label class="langsw"><span class="langsw-globe" aria-hidden="true">🌐</span><select data-langsw aria-label="Language" data-i18n="lang.label" data-i18n-attr="aria-label">
86	          <option value="en" data-i18n="lang.en" lang="en">English</option>
87	          <option value="kn" data-i18n="lang.kn" lang="kn">ಕನ್ನಡ</option>
88	          <option value="hi" data-i18n="lang.hi" lang="hi">हिन्दी</option>
89	        </select></label>
90	        <a href="/#join" style="color:#C7F2B0;font-weight:600;text-decoration:none" data-i18n="site.ownModel.requestAccess">Request access →</a>
91	      </div>
92	    </div>
93	    <h1>Annotate your footage. <span class="hl">Train a model that's yours.</span></h1>
94	    <p>You can't train Gemini or GPT on your court footage. KhelSutra's direction is the inverse — a small, <b style="color:#EAF3EE">owned</b> rally model where the moat is your consented dataset, on a commercially-clean, inspectable stack you control.</p>
95	    <div class="nav"><a href="/">← KhelSutra home</a><a href="#annotate">Annotate</a><a href="#train">Train your own model</a><a href="#setup">Setup &amp; use</a></div>
96	  </div>
97	
98	  <div class="body">
99	
100	    <p class="sample">▶ <b>Want to see what KhelSutra produces?</b> A real sample reel plays the moment you <a href="/#join">request access</a>.</p>
101	
102	    <section class="sec" id="annotate">
103	      <div class="kick">Step 1 · real and shipping today</div>
104	      <h2>Annotate your footage <span class="pill live">live today</span></h2>
105	      <p><b>rally-annotator</b> is a free, open-source (MIT) <b>VLC plugin</b> that turns "watch a match" into golden labels. While you watch your own video in VLC, you mark each rally's start and end plus how the point ended; it writes one row per rally to a plain CSV next to your video. <b>Your footage never leaves your machine.</b> It covers five net-separated racquet sports (badminton, tennis, table tennis, pickleball, padel).</p>
106	      <a class="ghbtn" href="https://github.com/Khelsutra/rally-annotator"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 0C3.58 0 0 3.58 0 8a8 8 0 0 0 5.47 7.59c.4.07.55-.17.55-.38 0-.19-.01-.82-.01-1.49-2.01.37-2.53-.49-2.69-.94-.09-.23-.48-.94-.82-1.13-.28-.15-.68-.52-.01-.53.63-.01 1.08.58 1.23.82.72 1.21 1.87.87 2.33.66.07-.52.28-.87.51-1.07-1.78-.2-3.64-.89-3.64-3.95 0-.87.31-1.59.82-2.15-.08-.2-.36-1.02.08-2.12 0 0 .67-.21 2.2.82a7.4 7.4 0 0 1 2-.27c.68 0 1.36.09 2 .27 1.53-1.04 2.2-.82 2.2-.82.44 1.1.16 1.92.08 2.12.51.56.82 1.27.82 2.15 0 3.07-1.87 3.75-3.65 3.95.29.25.54.73.54 1.48 0 1.07-.01 1.93-.01 2.2 0 .21.15.46.55.38A8 8 0 0 0 16 8c0-4.42-3.58-8-8-8Z"/></svg> github.com/Khelsutra/rally-annotator</a>
107	      <div class="shots">
108	        <figure><img src="/img/annotate-enable.png" alt="VLC View menu showing the Rally Annotator extension entry" loading="lazy" /><figcaption>Enable it once: VLC → View → Rally Annotator.</figcaption></figure>
109	        <figure><img src="/img/annotate-tool.png" alt="The Rally Annotator dialog — Mark START, Mark END, ending reason, Save Rally" loading="lazy" /><figcaption>Mark each rally's start/end + ending reason, then Save Rally.</figcaption></figure>
110	      </div>
111	      <p style="margin-top:1rem"><b>The flow, per rally:</b></p>
112	      <ol>
113	        <li><b>Mark START</b> on the serve-contact frame (pause/scrub for accuracy).</li>
114	        <li><b>Mark END</b> when the shuttle is dead.</li>
115	        <li>Pick an <b>ending reason</b>, optionally a shot count, then <b>Save Rally</b> — one CSV row.</li>
116	        <li>Fix anything with <b>Edit / Delete / Undo</b>; re-open the same video later to continue.</li>
117	      </ol>
118	      <p style="font-size:.9rem;color:var(--mut)">It's a click-driven dialog (no marking hotkeys yet), and targets <b>VLC 3.0.x</b>.</p>
119	    </section>
120	
121	    <section class="sec" id="train">
122	      <div class="kick">Step 2 · the moat</div>
123	      <h2>Train your own model <span class="pill build">open pipeline · in build</span></h2>
124	      <div class="honest" style="border-left-color:var(--green);background:#EAF6EF;border-color:#BfE6D2">
125	        <span aria-hidden="true" style="color:var(--green-d);font-size:1.2rem">✓</span>
126	        <div>
127	          <b>You don't have to train anything to start.</b>
128	          <p>KhelSutra runs on a ready-made default, so you get rally detection and reels <b>out of the box</b> — nothing to set up. Training on your own footage (below) is an <b>optional</b> power-up: a model tuned to your courts, your angles, your halls.</p>
129	        </div>
130	      </div>
131	      <p>For those who want it, your labels feed an <b>owned</b> rally model — trained on <b>your</b> courts, <b>your</b> angles, <b>your</b> busy multi-court halls. The pipeline is open and inspectable:</p>
132	      <pre><code>annotate (VLC)  →  &lt;video&gt;.rallies.csv  →  worker train  →  your model bundle (weights + a reviewable manifest)</code></pre>
133	      <div class="moat">
134	        <div><h4>You own it</h4><p>A model and dataset that are yours to keep and improve — not rented inference.</p></div>
135	        <div><h4>Commercially clean</h4><p>MIT/Apache/BSD-only stack; we never train on a paid LLM's output.</p></div>
136	        <div><h4>Private &amp; consented</h4><p>Footage stays yours; training use is explicit opt-in, never automatic.</p></div>
137	        <div><h4>Minors protected</h4><p>Footage involving minors is never used for training and never published.</p></div>
138	      </div>
139	      <div class="honest">
140	        <span aria-hidden="true" style="color:var(--green-d);font-size:1.2rem">!</span>
141	        <div>
142	          <b>Read this honestly.</b>
143	          <p>The labeling tool is real and shipping. The training pipeline is <b>open and in active build</b> — today it's a developer CLI; the no-GPU path trains a pipeline-validation <b>mock</b>, and a real model needs a GPU. There is <b>no "Train now" button</b> yet, and we make <b>no accuracy claim vs a paid LLM</b> (there's no such benchmark). The promise here is <b>ownership, licensing, privacy and consent</b> — not "more accurate than Gemini," and not "one click to train."</p>
144	        </div>
145	      </div>
146	    </section>
147	
148	    <section class="sec" id="setup">
149	      <div class="kick">Step 3 · for power users</div>
150	      <h2>Setup &amp; use</h2>
151	      <p><b>Install the plugin (VLC 3.0.x).</b> Copy <code>rally_annotator.lua</code> into VLC's per-user Lua <code>extensions</code> folder (create it if missing), then VLC → Tools → Plugins and extensions → <i>Reload extensions</i>, and enable it from <i>View → Rally Annotator</i>:</p>
152	      <pre><code>Windows:  %APPDATA%\vlc\lua\extensions\
153	macOS:    ~/Library/Application Support/org.videolan.vlc/lua/extensions/
154	Linux:    ~/.local/share/vlc/lua/extensions/</code></pre>
155	      <p style="margin-top:1rem"><b>Record</b> per the <a href="/capture">one-page capture checklist</a> (fixed tripod, full court, landscape, whole session) → <b>annotate</b> in VLC → you get <code>&lt;video&gt;.rallies.csv</code>:</p>
156	      <pre><code>rally_number,start_time,end_time,ending_reason,sport,shots_count
157	1,8.800,11.500,winner,badminton,9
158	2,24.389,46.589,unforced_error,badminton,21
159	3,49.183,54.683,let,badminton,</code></pre>
160	      <p style="margin-top:.6rem;font-size:.9rem;color:var(--mut)"><code>ending_reason</code> is one of: <code>unknown</code> · <code>winner</code> · <code>forced_error</code> · <code>unforced_error</code> · <code>service_fault</code> · <code>let</code> · <code>other</code>. Times are decimal seconds; downstream tools read by column name.</p>
161	      <p style="margin-top:1rem"><b>Train</b> (developer CLI). A GPU-free dry run validates the whole pipeline on a CPU mock; a real model needs a GPU:</p>
162	      <pre><code># GPU-free dry run (CPU mock — pipeline validation only):
163	python -m backend.worker train --corpus-uri &lt;bucket&gt; --out-uri &lt;bucket&gt; --version 0.1.0
164	
165	# real trajectories (needs a GPU + the native WASB runner, in build):
166	python -m backend.worker train ... --trajectory-source detector --segmenter wasb_hybrid</code></pre>
167	      <p style="margin-top:.6rem;font-size:.9rem;color:var(--mut)">One join rule: keep the video <b>byte-identical</b> to what you annotated (don't re-encode after labeling) — labels match videos by content hash.</p>
168	    </section>
169	
170	    <section class="sec">
171	      <div class="kick">So expectations stay honest</div>
172	      <h2>What this is not</h2>
173	      <ul class="nots">
174	        <li>Not a one-click trainer — training is an offline CLI today.</li>
175	        <li>Not in-browser — labeling runs in VLC on your machine.</li>
176	        <li>Not an accuracy claim vs Gemini/GPT — no such benchmark.</li>
177	        <li>Shot-type, player and score detection are roadmap.</li>
178	      </ul>
179	    </section>
180	
181	    <div class="cta">
182	      <b>Want in?</b>
183	      <div><a href="/#join">Request alpha access</a></div>
184	    </div>
185	
186	  </div>
187	  <div class="foot">
188	    <span><a href="/">khelsutra.guru</a> · <a href="https://github.com/Khelsutra/rally-annotator">rally-annotator (MIT)</a> · Academy flyer: <a href="/flyer" hreflang="en">English</a> · <a href="/flyer-kn" hreflang="kn" lang="kn">ಕನ್ನಡ</a> · <a href="/flyer-hi" hreflang="hi" lang="hi">हिन्दी</a></span>
189	    <span>Reels carry a "Generated by Khelsutra.guru" mark.</span>
190	  </div>
191	</div>
192	</body>
193	</html>

1	// KhelSutra marketing-site i18n shim (tracker B3) — vanilla, dependency-free, zero build.
2	//
3	// Mirrors the SPA's react-i18next contract (web/src/i18n/index.ts) for the static site:
4	//   - the SAME shared catalogs (repo-root /locales, copied into /public/locales — D-CATALOG-HOME);
5	//   - the SAME negotiation order: persisted (localStorage `khelsutra.lang`) -> ?lang= -> navigator -> en;
6	//   - ICU-lite interpolation `{name}` + plurals `{n, plural, one {…} other {…}}` via Intl.PluralRules
7	//     (CLDR-correct for en/kn/hi — the 2-form site languages — and any locale);
8	//   - a missing key degrades to en, then leaves the HTML's English default — NEVER a raw key.
9	//
10	// The pure helpers are exported for the unit test (src/i18n.shim.test.js); boot() runs only in a browser.
11	
12	export const SUPPORTED = ["en", "kn", "hi"]; // the trilingual marketing site (es/da/id are SPA-only)
13	export const STORAGE_KEY = "khelsutra.lang"; // shared with the SPA, so a choice carries across surfaces
14	export const DEFAULT = "en";
15	
16	const base = (code) => (code ? String(code).toLowerCase().split("-")[0] : ""); // "kn-IN" -> "kn"
17	const supported = (code) => (SUPPORTED.includes(base(code)) ? base(code) : null);
18	
19	// Negotiation — EXACTLY the SPA order. Anything unsupported is skipped; nothing matches -> en.
20	export function negotiate({ stored, query, nav } = {}) {
21	  const navList = Array.isArray(nav) ? nav : nav ? [nav] : [];
22	  for (const candidate of [stored, query, ...navList]) {
23	    const ok = supported(candidate);
24	    if (ok) return ok;
25	  }
26	  return DEFAULT;
27	}
28	
29	const dig = (obj, key) => key.split(".").reduce((o, k) => (o && typeof o === "object" ? o[k] : undefined), obj);
30	
31	// Resolve one plural block "{var, plural, one {…} other {…}}" (balanced braces, # = the number).
32	// Returns the string unchanged when there's no parseable plural block (so interpolate() terminates).
33	function plural(msg, vars, locale) {
34	  const m = msg.match(/\{(\w+),\s*plural,/);
35	  if (!m) return msg;
36	  let depth = 0, end = -1;
37	  for (let i = m.index; i < msg.length; i++) {
38	    if (msg[i] === "{") depth++;
39	    else if (msg[i] === "}" && --depth === 0) { end = i; break; }
40	  }
41	  if (end < 0) return msg;
42	  const inner = msg.slice(m.index + m[0].length, end);
43	  const n = Number(vars[m[1]] ?? 0);
44	  const cat = new Intl.PluralRules(locale).select(n);
45	  const arms = {};
46	  for (const a of inner.matchAll(/(\w+)\s*\{/g)) {
47	    let d = 1, j = a.index + a[0].length, e = -1;
48	    for (; j < inner.length; j++) { if (inner[j] === "{") d++; else if (inner[j] === "}" && --d === 0) { e = j; break; } }
49	    if (e < 0) break;
50	    arms[a[1]] = inner.slice(a.index + a[0].length, e);
51	  }
52	  const chosen = (arms[cat] ?? arms.other ?? "").replace(/#/g, String(n));
53	  return msg.slice(0, m.index) + chosen + msg.slice(end + 1);
54	}
55	
56	// ICU-lite: resolve every plural block, then simple {name} interpolation.
57	export function interpolate(msg, vars = {}, locale = DEFAULT) {
58	  if (typeof msg !== "string") return msg;
59	  let out = msg;
60	  for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) { const next = plural(out, vars, locale); if (next === out) break; out = next; }
61	  return out.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (_, k) => (k in vars ? String(vars[k]) : `{${k}}`));
62	}
63	
64	// active locale -> en fallback. undefined when absent in both (caller keeps the DOM's English default).
65	export function translate(catalogs, locale, key, vars) {
66	  let msg = dig(catalogs[locale], key);
67	  if (msg == null) msg = dig(catalogs[DEFAULT], key);
68	  return msg == null ? undefined : interpolate(msg, vars, locale);
69	}
70	
71	// Walk i18n-tagged nodes under `root`. Three forms (missing key -> skip, leaving the HTML default):
72	//   data-i18n="key"                      -> textContent
73	//   data-i18n="key" data-i18n-attr="x"   -> setAttribute(x, …)  (placeholder / aria-label / alt …)
74	//   data-i18n-html="key"                 -> innerHTML  (for catalog-authored inline markup like <b>;
75	//                                           values are our own content, never user input)
76	export function applyDom(root, catalogs, locale) {
77	  for (const el of root.querySelectorAll("[data-i18n], [data-i18n-html]")) {
78	    const htmlKey = el.getAttribute("data-i18n-html");
79	    const val = translate(catalogs, locale, htmlKey || el.getAttribute("data-i18n"));
80	    if (val == null) continue;
81	    const attr = el.getAttribute("data-i18n-attr");
82	    if (htmlKey) el.innerHTML = val;
83	    else if (attr) el.setAttribute(attr, val);
84	    else el.textContent = val;
85	  }
86	  // Reflect the active locale in the compact <select> language switcher(s).
87	  for (const sel of root.querySelectorAll("select[data-langsw]")) sel.value = locale;
88	}
89	
90	// ---- browser glue (skipped under the plain-node unit test, which has no document) ----
91	const readCookie = (name) => {
92	  const m = (document.cookie || "").match(new RegExp("(?:^|; )" + name.replace(/\./g, "\\.") + "=([^;]*)"));
93	  return m ? decodeURIComponent(m[1]) : null;
94	};
95	
96	function persist(locale) {
97	  try { localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, locale); } catch { /* private mode */ }
98	  // Mirror to a cookie so server.js (VM host) can honor the persisted pref server-side (no FOUC).
99	  try { document.cookie = `${STORAGE_KEY}=${locale};path=/;max-age=31536000;samesite=lax`; } catch { /* ignore */ }
100	}
101	
102	async function loadCatalog(loc) {
103	  try { const r = await fetch(`/locales/${loc}/common.json`); return r.ok ? await r.json() : {}; } catch { return {}; }
104	}
105	
106	let activeCatalogs = { en: {} };
107	let activeLocale = DEFAULT;
108	
109	async function render(locale) {
110	  const catalogs = { en: await loadCatalog("en") };
111	  if (locale !== "en") catalogs[locale] = await loadCatalog(locale);
112	  activeCatalogs = catalogs;
113	  activeLocale = locale;
114	  document.documentElement.lang = locale;
115	  applyDom(document, catalogs, locale);
116	  // Bridge for dynamically-injected content the static data-i18n walk can't reach (e.g. the
117	  // post-signup reel reveal): window.KhelSutra.t(key, vars) -> active-locale string (en fallback;
118	  // undefined if absent everywhere, so callers can supply their own default).
119	  if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
120	    window.KhelSutra = { t: (key, vars) => translate(activeCatalogs, activeLocale, key, vars), lang: activeLocale };
121	  }
122	}
123	
124	async function boot() {
125	  let stored = null;
126	  try { stored = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY); } catch { /* ignore */ }
127	  if (!stored) stored = readCookie(STORAGE_KEY); // server-set or cross-store persisted pref
128	  const query = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get("lang");
129	  const nav = navigator.languages && navigator.languages.length ? [...navigator.languages] : [navigator.language];
130	  const locale = negotiate({ stored, query, nav });
131	  await render(locale);
132	  document.addEventListener("change", (e) => {
133	    const sel = e.target.closest && e.target.closest("select[data-langsw]");
134	    if (!sel) return;
135	    const loc = supported(sel.value) || DEFAULT;
136	    persist(loc);
137	    render(loc);
138	  });
139	}
140	
141	if (typeof document !== "undefined" && typeof window !== "undefined") {
142	  if (document.readyState === "loading") document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", boot);
143	  else boot();
144	}

### 13. user (2026-06-22T01:11:55.009Z)

1	// DRIFT GUARD for the one build step the otherwise no-build site has (tracker B3 / D-CATALOG-HOME):
2	// the catalogs the marketing site ships (site/public/locales/) MUST be byte-identical to the single
3	// source of truth (repo-root /locales/). If someone edits /locales without re-running the copy, this
4	// fails CI with the exact fix. Run `npm run copy:locales` to resync.
5	import { test } from "node:test";
6	import assert from "node:assert/strict";
7	import { readFileSync, readdirSync } from "node:fs";
8	import { join } from "node:path";
9	
10	const SRC = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..", "locales");
11	const PUB = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "public", "locales");
12	
13	test("site/public/locales mirrors repo-root /locales exactly (else: npm run copy:locales)", () => {
14	  const locales = readdirSync(SRC, { withFileTypes: true }).filter((d) => d.isDirectory()).map((d) => d.name);
15	  assert.ok(locales.length >= 3, "expected at least en/kn/hi catalogs in /locales");
16	
17	  const shipped = new Set(readdirSync(PUB, { withFileTypes: true }).filter((d) => d.isDirectory()).map((d) => d.name));
18	  for (const loc of locales) {
19	    const src = readFileSync(join(SRC, loc, "common.json"), "utf8");
20	    assert.ok(shipped.has(loc), `site/public/locales/${loc} is missing — run npm run copy:locales`);
21	    const pub = readFileSync(join(PUB, loc, "common.json"), "utf8");
22	    assert.equal(pub, src, `site/public/locales/${loc}/common.json drifted from /locales/${loc} — run npm run copy:locales`);
23	  }
24	  // No stale extra locales shipped that aren't in the source of truth.
25	  for (const loc of shipped) {
26	    assert.ok(locales.includes(loc), `site/public/locales/${loc} has no source in /locales — run npm run copy:locales`);
27	  }
28	});
29

243	    // Order matters: the Latin display font leads, then the Noto Indic families, BEFORE the generic
244	    // fallbacks — else a system UI font with partial Indic glyphs could win and defeat the bundle.
245	    const stack = html.match(/--font:([^;}]*)/)[1];
246	    assert.ok(stack.indexOf("'Noto Sans Kannada'") < stack.indexOf("system-ui"), `${f}: Noto Kannada must precede system-ui`);
247	    assert.ok(stack.indexOf("'Noto Sans Devanagari'") < stack.indexOf("system-ui"), `${f}: Noto Devanagari must precede system-ui`);

274	test("A3: EVERY data-i18n[-html] HTML default matches the en catalog byte-for-byte (no drift, incl. rich <b> keys)", () => {
275	  // The rich (data-i18n-html) keys are the most drift-prone: the inline <b> markup must match the
276	  // catalog exactly, or an en visitor's no-JS default and the shim's en render silently diverge.
277	  // Parse the REAL pages in jsdom and compare each tagged element's default to enCatalog.
278	  const dig = (o, k) => k.split(".").reduce((a, c) => (a == null ? a : a[c]), o);
279	  let checked = 0;
280	  for (const f of ["index.html", "capture.html", "own-model.html"]) {
281	    const { document } = new JSDOM(read(f)).window;
282	    for (const el of document.querySelectorAll("[data-i18n], [data-i18n-html]")) {
283	      if (el.getAttribute("data-i18n-attr")) continue; // attribute defaults aren't the visible text
284	      const htmlKey = el.getAttribute("data-i18n-html");
285	      const key = htmlKey || el.getAttribute("data-i18n");
286	      const enVal = dig(enCatalog, key);
287	      assert.notEqual(enVal, undefined, `${f}: data-i18n key "${key}" missing from en catalog`);
288	      const got = (htmlKey ? el.innerHTML : el.textContent).trim();
289	      assert.equal(got, enVal, `${f}: "${key}" HTML default drifted from the en catalog`);
290	      checked++;
291	    }
292	  }
293	  assert.ok(checked >= 60, `expected to check the full localized surface, only saw ${checked}`);
294	});

1	// Unit tests for the marketing-site i18n shim (public/i18n.js, tracker B3). Proves the shim mirrors
2	// the SPA's react-i18next contract: same negotiation order, ICU-lite interpolation/plurals, en
3	// fallback, and a data-i18n walker that NEVER leaves a raw key. Tested against the REAL shared
4	// catalogs (/locales) — so this also proves both surfaces consume one source of truth.
5	import { test } from "node:test";
6	import assert from "node:assert/strict";
7	import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
8	import { join } from "node:path";
9	import { JSDOM } from "jsdom";
10	import { negotiate, interpolate, translate, applyDom, SUPPORTED, DEFAULT } from "../public/i18n.js";
11	
12	const LOCALES = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..", "locales");
13	const load = (loc) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(LOCALES, loc, "common.json"), "utf8"));
14	const catalogs = { en: load("en"), kn: load("kn"), hi: load("hi") };
15	
16	test("negotiation order matches the SPA: persisted -> ?lang -> navigator -> en", () => {
17	  assert.equal(negotiate({ stored: "kn", query: "hi", nav: ["en-US"] }), "kn", "persisted pref wins");
18	  assert.equal(negotiate({ query: "hi", nav: ["en-US"] }), "hi", "?lang beats navigator");
19	  assert.equal(negotiate({ nav: ["kn-IN", "en-IN"] }), "kn", "navigator picks the first supported");
20	  assert.equal(negotiate({}), "en", "nothing -> en");
21	});
22	
23	test("region subtags normalize and unsupported degrades to en (never a locale we can't serve)", () => {
24	  assert.equal(negotiate({ nav: ["hi-IN"] }), "hi", "hi-IN -> hi");
25	  assert.equal(negotiate({ nav: ["ta-IN"] }), "en", "Tamil (unsupported) -> en");
26	  assert.equal(negotiate({ stored: "es" }), "en", "es is SPA-only — the trilingual site degrades it to en");
27	  assert.deepEqual([...SUPPORTED], ["en", "kn", "hi"]);
28	  assert.equal(DEFAULT, "en");
29	});
30	
31	test("ICU-lite interpolation: {name} substitution", () => {
32	  assert.equal(interpolate("over {n}s", { n: 10 }), "over 10s");
33	  assert.equal(interpolate("Couldn't load: {error}", { error: "boom" }), "Couldn't load: boom");
34	});
35	
36	test("ICU-lite plurals via Intl.PluralRules (en/kn 2-form), # = the number", () => {
37	  const en = catalogs.en.selection.count; // "{count, plural, one {# rally selected} other {# rallies selected}}"
38	  assert.equal(interpolate(en, { count: 1 }, "en"), "1 rally selected");
39	  assert.equal(interpolate(en, { count: 5 }, "en"), "5 rallies selected");
40	  // Kannada is also one/other — the shim picks the arm from the catalog, no hard-coded rules.
41	  assert.equal(translate(catalogs, "kn", "selection.count", { count: 1 }), catalogs.kn.selection.count.match(/one \{([^}]*)\}/)[1].replace("#", "1"));
42	});

44	test("translate: active locale, then en fallback, then undefined (caller keeps the DOM default)", () => {
45	  assert.equal(translate(catalogs, "kn", "app.title"), catalogs.kn.app.title); // real Kannada
46	  assert.equal(translate(catalogs, "en", "lang.kn"), "ಕನ್ನಡ"); // shared catalog autonym
47	  // A3: site.* marketing is now translated for kn (no longer the en seed).

49	  assert.notEqual(catalogs.kn.site.capture.title, catalogs.en.site.capture.title, "kn capture.title must be translated, not an en seed");
50	  // en fallback when a key is absent from the active locale (synthetic — real catalogs have full parity).

52	  assert.equal(translate(catalogs, "kn", "does.not.exist"), undefined, "missing everywhere -> undefined");

57	    <span id="auto" data-i18n="lang.kn">PLACEHOLDER</span>

61	      <option value="en" data-i18n="lang.en">English</option>
62	      <option value="kn" data-i18n="lang.kn">ಕನ್ನಡ</option>
63	      <option value="hi" data-i18n="lang.hi">हिन्दी</option>
64	    </select>
65	  </body>`).window;

67	  applyDom(document, catalogs, "kn");
68	
69	  assert.equal(document.getElementById("auto").textContent, "ಕನ್ನಡ", "data-i18n sets textContent from the shared catalog");
70	  assert.equal(document.getElementById("keep").textContent, "KEEP ME", "a missing key leaves the HTML default — never a raw key");
71	  assert.equal(document.getElementById("ph").getAttribute("placeholder"), catalogs.kn.site.capture.title, "data-i18n-attr targets an attribute (kn value for the kn locale)");
72	  // The compact <select> reflects the active locale (its displayed text follows the selected option).

75	  assert.equal(sw.selectedOptions[0].textContent, "ಕನ್ನಡ", "the selected option shows the localized autonym");
76	});

78	test("data-i18n-html sets innerHTML and preserves inline <b> markup (A3 recording-education)", () => {
79	  const { document } = new JSDOM(`<!doctype html><body>
80	    <p id="rich" data-i18n-html="site.record.place.body">Behind <b>one baseline</b> …</p>
81	    <li id="kept" data-i18n-html="does.not.exist">KEEP <b>this</b></li>
82	  </body>`).window;
83	
84	  // en: innerHTML equals the catalog value verbatim (with its <b> tags), not escaped text.
85	  applyDom(document, catalogs, "en");
86	  assert.equal(document.getElementById("rich").innerHTML, catalogs.en.site.record.place.body);
87	  assert.ok(document.getElementById("rich").querySelector("b"), "the <b> must be a real element, not escaped text");
88	  assert.equal(document.getElementById("kept").innerHTML, "KEEP <b>this</b>", "a missing html key leaves the HTML default");
89	
90	  // kn: swaps to the Kannada value, still carrying its <b> tags.
91	  applyDom(document, catalogs, "kn");
92	  assert.equal(document.getElementById("rich").innerHTML, catalogs.kn.site.record.place.body);
93	  assert.match(document.getElementById("rich").innerHTML, /<b>.*<\/b>/s, "kn rich value keeps <b> markup");
94	});
95	
96	test("data-i18n-html falls back through en (markup intact) when the active locale lacks the key", () => {
97	  // The middle rung: active locale missing a rich key -> en value (with <b>), not just the DOM default.
98	  const { document } = new JSDOM(`<!doctype html><body><p id="r" data-i18n-html="site.r.b">default</p></body>`).window;
99	  applyDom(document, { en: { site: { r: { b: "X <b>Y</b> Z" } } }, kn: {} }, "kn");
100	  assert.equal(document.getElementById("r").innerHTML, "X <b>Y</b> Z", "html form must fall back to en, not the DOM default");
101	  assert.ok(document.getElementById("r").querySelector("b"), "the <b> survives the en-fallback path");
102	});

104	test("A3: the kn/hi catalogs carry REAL recording-education translations (not English seeds)", () => {
105	  // Kannada script range U+0C80–U+0CFF; Devanagari U+0900–U+097F. A representative spread of the

107	  const KN = /[ಀ-೿]/, HI = /[ऀ-ॿ]/;
108	  for (const key of ["hero.title", "record.intro", "record.place.title", "capture.step1.b3", "capture.consent", "capture.diagram.camera", "nav.requestAccess"]) {
109	    const path = key.split(".");

1	// Mirror the single-source-of-truth catalogs (repo-root /locales, D-CATALOG-HOME) into the site's
2	// static public/ dir, so the dependency-free marketing site can fetch them at runtime via the
3	// t()/data-i18n shim (public/i18n.js). This copy is the ONLY build step the otherwise no-build site
4	// has. Run on demand (`npm run copy:locales`) or automatically before deploy (`predeploy`).
5	//
6	// The committed copy under site/public/locales/ is what actually ships (static hosts serve it with
7	// no build); the drift-guard test (src/locales-drift.test.js) fails CI if it goes stale vs /locales.
8	import { cpSync, rmSync, mkdirSync, readdirSync } from "node:fs";
9	import { join } from "node:path";
10	
11	const SRC = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..", "locales");
12	const DEST = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "public", "locales");
13	
14	rmSync(DEST, { recursive: true, force: true });
15	mkdirSync(DEST, { recursive: true });
16	cpSync(SRC, DEST, { recursive: true });
17	
18	console.log(`copy-locales: ${readdirSync(DEST).join(", ")} -> site/public/locales/`);

1	// CI guardrails that keep i18n "by design" (FRIENDLY_AND_MULTILINGUAL.md §4):
2	//   1. KEY-PARITY — every locale catalog has EXACTLY the `en` key set (the extensibility proof:
3	//      drop a stub locale and this lists every gap). Trivially green while only `en` exists; it
4	//      starts enforcing the moment a kn/hi catalog lands (B5/B6).
5	//   2. NO-BARE-STRINGS (attribute level, v1) — user-facing component attributes (placeholder /
6	//      aria-label / title) must route through t(), never a hardcoded literal. A curated start per
7	//      the doc; an AST-level sweep of JSX text can extend it later.
8	import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
9	import { readdirSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";
10	import { join } from "node:path";
11	
12	// Catalogs are hoisted to repo-root /locales (D-CATALOG-HOME): web/src/i18n -> ../../../locales.
13	const LOCALES_DIR = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..", "..", "locales");
14	const SRC_DIR = join(import.meta.dirname, "..");
15	
16	function flatten(obj: Record<string, unknown>, prefix = ""): string[] {
17	  const keys: string[] = [];
18	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(obj)) {
19	    const key = prefix ? `${prefix}.${k}` : k;
20	    if (v && typeof v === "object" && !Array.isArray(v)) keys.push(...flatten(v as Record<string, unknown>, key));
21	    else keys.push(key);
22	  }
23	  return keys.sort();
24	}
25	
26	const loadKeys = (locale: string): string[] =>
27	  flatten(JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(LOCALES_DIR, locale, "common.json"), "utf8")) as Record<string, unknown>);
28	
29	describe("i18n key-parity — every locale has EXACTLY the en keys", () => {
30	  const enKeys = loadKeys("en");
31	  const others = readdirSync(LOCALES_DIR, { withFileTypes: true })
32	    .filter((d) => d.isDirectory() && d.name !== "en")
33	    .map((d) => d.name);
34	
35	  it("en is the non-empty source catalog", () => {
36	    expect(enKeys.length).toBeGreaterThan(20);
37	  });
38	
39	  for (const loc of others) {
40	    it(`${loc} matches en exactly (no missing, no extra keys)`, () => {
41	      const keys = loadKeys(loc);
42	      const missing = enKeys.filter((k) => !keys.includes(k));
43	      const extra = keys.filter((k) => !enKeys.includes(k));
44	      expect({ locale: loc, missing, extra }).toEqual({ locale: loc, missing: [], extra: [] });
45	    });
46	  }
47	});
48	
49	// Key-parity proves every locale has the same KEYS; this proves every locale's VALUE keeps the same
50	// simple `{placeholder}` set as en — so a translation can never silently drop a {count}/{error}/{min}/
51	// {filename}/{n}/… and break interpolation. (ICU plural headers like `{count, plural, …}` carry no
52	// simple {word} placeholder and match as empty on both sides, so they're correctly ignored.)
53	describe("placeholder parity — every locale preserves en's {placeholders}", () => {
54	  function flatEntries(obj: Record<string, unknown>, prefix = ""): Record<string, string> {
55	    const out: Record<string, string> = {};
56	    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(obj)) {
57	      const key = prefix ? `${prefix}.${k}` : k;
58	      if (v && typeof v === "object" && !Array.isArray(v)) Object.assign(out, flatEntries(v as Record<string, unknown>, key));
59	      else out[key] = String(v);
60	    }
61	    return out;
62	  }
63	  const placeholders = (s: string) => (s.match(/\{(\w+)\}/g) ?? []).sort();
64	  const load = (loc: string) => flatEntries(JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(LOCALES_DIR, loc, "common.json"), "utf8")));
65	  const enE = load("en");
66	  const others = readdirSync(LOCALES_DIR, { withFileTypes: true })
67	    .filter((d) => d.isDirectory() && d.name !== "en")
68	    .map((d) => d.name);
69	
70	  for (const loc of others) {
71	    it(`${loc} preserves every {placeholder} that en uses`, () => {
72	      const locE = load(loc);
73	      const mismatches: Array<{ key: string; en: string[]; got: string[] }> = [];
74	      for (const key of Object.keys(enE)) {
75	        if (!(key in locE)) continue; // missing keys are the key-parity test's job
76	        const a = placeholders(enE[key]);
77	        const b = placeholders(locE[key]);
78	        if (a.join(",") !== b.join(",")) mismatches.push({ key, en: a, got: b });
79	      }
80	      expect(mismatches).toEqual([]);
81	    });
82	  }
83	});
84	
85	describe("es 3-form plural (CLDR one/many/other) — MULTILINGUAL Phase A", () => {
86	  // Spanish cardinal is {one, many, other}; every es ICU plural message MUST carry a `many {` arm
87	  // (the `many` category fires on large/compact numbers) or intl-messageformat silently falls back.
88	  // These are the plural-bearing keys in the catalog.
89	  const es = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(LOCALES_DIR, "es", "common.json"), "utf8")) as Record<string, Record<string, string>>;
90	  const pluralKeys: Array<[string, string]> = [
91	    ["footer", "count"],
92	    ["footer", "floorSomeLead"],
93	    ["selection", "count"],
94	  ];
95	  for (const [ns, key] of pluralKeys) {
96	    it(`es ${ns}.${key} carries one/many/other arms`, () => {
97	      const msg = es[ns][key];
98	      expect(msg, `${ns}.${key}`).toMatch(/\bone\s*\{/);
99	      expect(msg, `${ns}.${key}`).toMatch(/\bmany\s*\{/);
100	      expect(msg, `${ns}.${key}`).toMatch(/\bother\s*\{/);
101	    });
102	  }
103	});
104	
105	describe("no-bare-strings — user-facing component attributes go through t()", () => {
106	  const FILES = [
107	    "App.tsx",
108	    "components/QueryBar.tsx",
109	    "components/TapFilters.tsx",
110	    "components/RallyGrid.tsx",
111	    "components/SelectionFooter.tsx",
112	    "components/CompileResult.tsx",
113	    "components/LanguageSwitcher.tsx",
114	    "components/IngestPanel.tsx",
115	  ];
116	  // A hardcoded placeholder / aria-label / title with letters is a bare-string regression — it
117	  // must be an expression ({t(...)}), not a quoted literal.
118	  const BARE_ATTR = /\b(?:placeholder|aria-label|title)\s*=\s*"[^"]*[A-Za-z]{2}[^"]*"/g;
119	
120	  for (const f of FILES) {
121	    it(`${f}: no hardcoded placeholder/aria-label/title`, () => {
122	      const src = readFileSync(join(SRC_DIR, f), "utf8");
123	      expect(src.match(BARE_ATTR) ?? []).toEqual([]);
124	    });
125	  }
126	});
127	
128	describe("Indic fonts (B4) — self-hosted OFL Noto, no runtime CDN", () => {
129	  it("main.tsx bundles the OFL Noto Kannada + Devanagari fonts (self-hosted, not Google Fonts)", () => {
130	    const main = readFileSync(join(SRC_DIR, "main.tsx"), "utf8");
131	    expect(main).toMatch(/@fontsource-variable\/noto-sans-kannada/);
132	    expect(main).toMatch(/@fontsource-variable\/noto-sans-devanagari/);
133	  });
134	  it("the font stack falls back to Noto for Kannada + Devanagari glyphs", () => {
135	    const css = readFileSync(join(SRC_DIR, "index.css"), "utf8");
136	    expect(css).toMatch(/Noto Sans Kannada Variable/);
137	    expect(css).toMatch(/Noto Sans Devanagari Variable/);
138	  });
139	});
140	
141	// The Studio SPA must render correctly on an AIR-GAPPED appliance (GPU/CPU/RAM, no internet) —
142	// every asset (fonts, styles, scripts) is bundled local, nothing pulled from a CDN/Google Fonts.
143	describe("offline / self-contained — no external runtime assets", () => {
144	  const CDN = /googleapis|gstatic|fonts\.google|cdnjs|unpkg|jsdelivr|\bcdn\.[a-z]/i;
145	  const FILES = [
146	    "../index.html", // web/index.html
147	    "main.tsx",
148	    "index.css",
149	  ];
150	  for (const rel of FILES) {
151	    it(`${rel}: no external font/CDN reference`, () => {
152	      expect(readFileSync(join(SRC_DIR, rel), "utf8")).not.toMatch(CDN);
153	    });
154	  }
155	  it("web/index.html loads no absolute-URL <link>/<script src> (everything is bundled/local)", () => {
156	    const html = readFileSync(join(SRC_DIR, "..", "index.html"), "utf8");
157	    expect(html).not.toMatch(/(?:href|src)\s*=\s*["']https?:\/\//i);
158	  });
159	});

1	// Records two screencasts of the localized + decluttered marketing site:
2	//   1) desktop multilingual journey: home (en) -> compact switcher -> Kannada home -> capture page
3	//   2) mobile header: the clean compact switcher reflowing en -> kn -> hi (no overflow)
4	// Run against the site server (URL env). Outputs .webm into ./ (Playwright records webm natively).
5	import { chromium } from "playwright";
6	import { rename } from "node:fs/promises";
7	import { mkdirSync } from "node:fs";
8	
9	const URL = process.env.URL || "http://localhost:8123";
10	const HOLD = 1600;
11	mkdirSync("rec", { recursive: true });
12	const browser = await chromium.launch();
13	
14	async function record(name, viewport, steps) {
15	  const ctx = await browser.newContext({ viewport, locale: "en-US", recordVideo: { dir: "rec", size: viewport } });
16	  const page = await ctx.newPage();
17	  await steps(page);
18	  const v = page.video();
19	  await ctx.close(); // flush the video
20	  await rename(await v.path(), name);
21	  console.log("wrote", name);
22	}
23	
24	// 1) Desktop: en home -> switch to Kannada -> recording gist -> capture page (kn persists)
25	await record("khelsutra-site-i18n-desktop.webm", { width: 1280, height: 800 }, async (page) => {
26	  await page.goto(URL + "/", { waitUntil: "domcontentloaded" });
27	  await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
28	  await page.selectOption("select[data-langsw]", "kn");
29	  await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
30	  await page.locator("#record").scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
31	  await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
32	  await page.goto(URL + "/capture", { waitUntil: "domcontentloaded" });
33	  await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
34	  await page.locator(".diagram").scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
35	  await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
36	});
37	
38	// 2) Mobile: the compact header switching en -> kn -> hi, never overflowing
39	await record("khelsutra-site-header-mobile.webm", { width: 390, height: 844 }, async (page) => {
40	  await page.goto(URL + "/", { waitUntil: "domcontentloaded" });

1	// Always-on VM host for the KhelSutra site — dependency-free (Node 22+ only).
2	// Serves public/ and POST /api/request-access into SQLite (node:sqlite), using the
3	// SAME handler logic as the Cloudflare Worker (src/index.js). This is the portability
4	// fallback "if Cloudflare acts out" — see docs/PORTABILITY.md.
5	//
6	//   PORT=8080 DB_PATH=/var/lib/khelsutra/access.db node server.js
7	//
8	import { createServer } from "node:http";
9	import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
10	import { join, extname, normalize } from "node:path";
11	import { DatabaseSync } from "node:sqlite";
12	import { handleRequest } from "./src/index.js";
13	import { sqliteStore } from "./src/store.js";
14	import { noopNotify } from "./src/notify.js";
15	
16	const PORT = Number(process.env.PORT) || 8080;
17	const ROOT = import.meta.dirname;
18	const PUBLIC = join(ROOT, "public");
19	const DB_PATH = process.env.DB_PATH || join(ROOT, "access.db");
20	
21	const db = new DatabaseSync(DB_PATH);
22	db.exec(await readFile(join(ROOT, "schema.sql"), "utf8")); // CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS — idempotent
23	const store = sqliteStore(db);
24	const notify = noopNotify(); // VM host: no email yet (add SMTP later — see docs/PORTABILITY.md)
25	
26	const TYPES = {
27	  ".html": "text/html; charset=utf-8", ".png": "image/png", ".svg": "image/svg+xml",
28	  ".css": "text/css", ".js": "text/javascript", ".json": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
29	  ".ico": "image/x-icon", ".txt": "text/plain",
30	  ".mp4": "video/mp4", ".webm": "video/webm", ".gif": "image/gif", ".jpg": "image/jpeg", ".jpeg": "image/jpeg",
31	};
32	
33	// --- Language negotiation (tracker B3 / §6.5 N8) ---------------------------------------------------
34	// The trilingual marketing site. The client shim (public/i18n.js) does the full negotiation in the
35	// browser; this VM host PRE-RESOLVES the locale server-side so the first paint (and any no-JS read)
36	// already carries the right <html lang>. Same ORDER as the SPA + shim: persisted pref -> ?lang= ->
37	// navigator/Accept-Language -> en. (On Cloudflare, assets are served directly and only the client
38	// shim negotiates — see src/index.js.)
39	const SITE_LOCALES = ["en", "kn", "hi"];
40	const LANG_COOKIE = "khelsutra.lang";
41	const LOCALIZED_PAGES = new Set(["index.html", "capture.html", "own-model.html"]);
42	
43	const baseLang = (code) => (code ? String(code).toLowerCase().split(";")[0].trim().split("-")[0] : "");
44	
45	function cookieValue(header, name) {
46	  const m = (header || "").match(new RegExp("(?:^|;\\s*)" + name.replace(/\./g, "\\.") + "=([^;]*)"));
47	  return m ? decodeURIComponent(m[1]) : null;
48	}
49	
50	function parseAcceptLanguage(header) {
51	  return (header || "")
52	    .split(",")
53	    .map((part) => {
54	      const [tag, ...params] = part.trim().split(";");
55	      const q = params.map((p) => p.trim()).find((p) => p.startsWith("q="));
56	      return { tag, q: q ? Number(q.slice(2)) : 1 };
57	    })
58	    .filter((x) => x.tag)
59	    .sort((a, b) => b.q - a.q)
60	    .map((x) => x.tag);
61	}
62	
63	// persisted (cookie) -> ?lang= -> Accept-Language -> en. Anything unsupported is skipped.
64	function negotiateLang(req, searchParams) {
65	  const candidates = [
66	    cookieValue(req.headers.cookie, LANG_COOKIE),
67	    searchParams.get("lang"),
68	    ...parseAcceptLanguage(req.headers["accept-language"]),
69	  ];
70	  for (const c of candidates) {
71	    const b = baseLang(c);
72	    if (b && SITE_LOCALES.includes(b)) return b;
73	  }
74	  return "en";
75	}
76	
77	const setHtmlLang = (html, lang) => html.replace(/<html lang="[^"]*"/i, `<html lang="${lang}"`);
78	
79	async function toRequest(req) {
80	  const url = `http://${req.headers.host || "localhost"}${req.url}`;
81	  let body;
82	  if (req.method !== "GET" && req.method !== "HEAD") {
83	    const chunks = [];
84	    for await (const c of req) chunks.push(c);
85	    body = Buffer.concat(chunks);
86	  }
87	  return new Request(url, { method: req.method, headers: req.headers, body });
88	}
89	
90	async function send(res, response) {
91	  res.statusCode = response.status;
92	  for (const [k, v] of response.headers) res.setHeader(k, v);
93	  res.end(Buffer.from(await response.arrayBuffer()));
94	}
95	
96	async function serveStatic(pathname, req, searchParams, res) {
97	  const rel = pathname === "/" ? "index.html"
98	    : pathname === "/capture" ? "capture.html"
99	    : pathname === "/own-model" ? "own-model.html"
100	    : pathname === "/flyer" ? "flyer.html"
101	    : pathname === "/flyer-kn" ? "flyer-kn.html"
102	    : pathname === "/flyer-hi" ? "flyer-hi.html"
103	    : pathname.replace(/^\/+/, "");
104	  const file = normalize(join(PUBLIC, rel));
105	  if (!file.startsWith(PUBLIC)) { res.statusCode = 403; return res.end("forbidden"); }
106	  try {
107	    let data = await readFile(file);
108	    res.setHeader("content-type", TYPES[extname(file)] || "application/octet-stream");
109	    if (LOCALIZED_PAGES.has(rel)) {
110	      // Pre-resolve the locale and stamp <html lang> so the first paint is correct (the client shim
111	      // refines it). Vary on Accept-Language so a shared cache keeps per-language copies distinct.
112	      const lang = negotiateLang(req, searchParams);
113	      if (lang !== "en") data = Buffer.from(setHtmlLang(data.toString("utf8"), lang), "utf8");
114	      res.setHeader("content-language", lang);
115	      res.setHeader("vary", "Accept-Language");
116	    }
117	    res.end(data);
118	  } catch {
119	    res.statusCode = 404;
120	    res.end("not found");
121	  }
122	}
123	
124	createServer(async (req, res) => {
125	  try {
126	    const parsed = new URL(`http://x${req.url}`);
127	    const pathname = decodeURIComponent(parsed.pathname);
128	    if (pathname === "/api/request-access") {
129	      if (req.method !== "POST") { res.statusCode = 405; return res.end('{"ok":false,"error":"method not allowed"}'); }
130	      return await send(res, await handleRequest(await toRequest(req), store, notify));
131	    }
132	    return await serveStatic(pathname, req, parsed.searchParams, res);
133	  } catch (err) {
134	    res.statusCode = 500;
135	    res.end('{"ok":false,"error":"server error"}');
136	    console.error(err);
137	  }
138	}).listen(PORT, () => console.log(`khelsutra site listening on :${PORT}  (sqlite: ${DB_PATH})`));
139

### 57. assistant (2026-06-22T01:23:00.968Z)

// One-off authoring helper (NOT shipped): extract the en catalog values for the own-model page
// straight from the wired HTML so they match the drift guard byte-for-byte. Walks every
// data-i18n[-html] node under site.ownModel.* and prints the nested object as JSON.
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { JSDOM } from "jsdom";

const html = readFileSync(join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "public", "own-model.html"), "utf8");
const { document } = new JSDOM(html).window;

const out = {};
const setDeep = (obj, path, val) => {
  const parts = path.split(".");
  let o = obj;
  for (let i = 0; i < parts.length - 1; i++) o = o[parts[i] ??= {}] ?? (o[parts[i]] = {});
  // rebuild because ??= above is wrong for nested — do it explicitly:
};
function put(root, dotted, value) {
  const parts = dotted.split(".");
  let o = root;
  for (let i = 0; i < parts.length - 1; i++) {
    o[parts[i]] = o[parts[i]] || {};
    o = o[parts[i]];
  }
  o[parts[parts.length - 1]] = value;
}

let n = 0;
for (const el of document.querySelectorAll("[data-i18n], [data-i18n-html]")) {
  if (el.getAttribute("data-i18n-attr")) continue; // attribute defaults aren't the visible text
  const htmlKey = el.getAttribute("data-i18n-html");
  const key = htmlKey || el.getAttribute("data-i18n");
  if (!key.startsWith("site.ownModel.")) continue;
  const value = (htmlKey ? el.innerHTML : el.textContent).trim();
  put(out, key, value);
  n++;
}
console.error(`extracted ${n} site.ownModel.* keys`);
process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(out.site.ownModel, null, 2) + "\n");

### 63. assistant (2026-06-22T01:24:20.503Z)

// One-off authoring helper (NOT shipped): seed site.ownModel.* into every catalog.
//  - en (authoritative) + es/da/id: the values extracted from the wired own-model.html.
//  - kn/hi: same en seed for the NEW keys, but PRESERVE any already-translated key
//    (requestAccess shipped in B5/B6) so we never clobber prior work. The kn/hi BODY
//    translations are applied separately (translate-ownmodel output) on top of this seed.
// Re-runnable: extraction is the single source of truth (parsed from the HTML byte-for-byte).
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { JSDOM } from "jsdom";

const ROOT = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..");
const html = readFileSync(join(ROOT, "site", "public", "own-model.html"), "utf8");
const { document } = new JSDOM(html).window;

function put(root, dotted, value) {
  const parts = dotted.split(".");
  let o = root;
  for (let i = 0; i < parts.length - 1; i++) o = o[parts[i]] = o[parts[i]] || {};
  o[parts[parts.length - 1]] = value;
}
const en = {};
for (const el of document.querySelectorAll("[data-i18n], [data-i18n-html]")) {
  if (el.getAttribute("data-i18n-attr")) continue;
  const htmlKey = el.getAttribute("data-i18n-html");
  const key = htmlKey || el.getAttribute("data-i18n");
  if (!key.startsWith("site.ownModel.")) continue;
  put(en, key, (htmlKey ? el.innerHTML : el.textContent).trim());
}
const enOwn = en.site.ownModel;

for (const loc of ["en", "kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"]) {
  const p = join(ROOT, "locales", loc, "common.json");
  const cat = JSON.parse(readFileSync(p, "utf8"));
  const prev = (cat.site && cat.site.ownModel) || {};
  const next = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(enOwn));
  if (loc === "kn" || loc === "hi") {
    // keep already-translated leaves (today: requestAccess); seed the rest with en
    if (prev.requestAccess) next.requestAccess = prev.requestAccess;
  }
  cat.site.ownModel = next;
  writeFileSync(p, JSON.stringify(cat, null, 2) + "\n");
  console.log(`${loc}: site.ownModel set (${Object.keys(next).length} top-level keys; requestAccess="${next.requestAccess}")`);
}

body{margin:0;font-family:var(--font);color:var(--tx);background:#E9EDE6;line-height:1.6;-webkit-font-smoothing:antialiased}\n  a{color:var(--green-d)}\n  .sheet{max-width:880px;margin:18px auto;background:#fff;border-radius:16px;overflow:hidden;box-shadow:0 18px 50px -24px rgba(0,0,0,.35)}\n  .head{background:var(--ink);color:#EAF3EE;padding:24px 32px}\n  .head .top{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:1rem;flex-wrap:wrap}\n  .brand{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.5rem;font-weight:800;font-size:1.2rem;color:#fff;text-decoration:none}\n  .brand .play{display:inline-block;width:0;height:0;border-style:solid;border-width:6px 0 6px 11px;border-color:transparent transparent transparent var(--green);margin:0 .3em;transform:translateY(1px)}\n  .head h1{font-size:clamp(1.5rem,3.5vw,2.1rem);font-weight:800;letter-spacing:-.02em;margin:1rem 0 0;max-width:20ch}\n  .head h1 .hl{color:var(--lime)}\n  .head p{color:#A7C3B7;margin:.7rem 0 0;max-width:62ch}\n  .head .nav{display:flex;gap:1rem;font-size:.9rem;margin-top:1rem;flex-wrap:wrap}\n  .head .nav a{color:var(--lime);text-decoration:none}\n  /* persistent language switcher (B3) — inherits header text colour, green fill = active */\n  .nav-right{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:1rem}\n  .langsw{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:.3rem;border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.28);border-radius:999px;padding:.1rem .3rem .1rem .6rem}\n  .langsw-globe{font-size:.95rem;line-height:1;opacity:.85}\n  .langsw select{font:inherit;font-size:.85rem;font-weight:600;color:inherit;background:transparent;border:0;cursor:pointer;min-height:40px}\n  .langsw select:focus-visible{outline:2px solid var(--green);outline-offset:2px;border-radius:6px}\n  .langsw option{color:#14271F;background:#fff}\n  .body{padding:26px 32px 8px}\n  .sec{padding:18px 0;border-top:1px solid var(--line)}\n  .sec:first-child{border-top:0}\n  .kick{font-size:.78rem;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--green-d)}\n  .sec h2{font-size:1.4rem;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:-.01em;margin:.35rem 0 0}\n  .sec p{color:#34453d;margin:.6rem 0 0}\n  .pill{display:inline-block;font-size:.72rem;font-weight:700;border-radius:999px;padding:.2rem .6rem;margin-left:.4rem;vertical-align:.12em}\n  .pill.live{background:rgba(25,179,107,.14);color:#0a6c45;border:1px solid rgba(25,179,107,.35)}\n  .pill.build{background:#FAEEDA;color:#854F0B;border:1px solid #EF9F27}\n  ol,ul{margin:.6rem 0 0;padding-left:1.2rem}\n  li{margin:.25rem 0;color:#34453d}\n  li b{color:var(--tx)}\n  code{font-family:ui-monospace,SFMono-Regular,Menlo,Consolas,monospace;font-size:.86em;background:#EEF3EC;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:5px;padding:.05rem .35rem}\n  pre{background:#0C261F;color:#EAF3EE;border-radius:10px;padding:14px 16px;overflow:auto;margin:.8rem 0 0;font-size:.84rem;line-height:1.5}\n  pre code{background:none;border:0;padding:0;color:inherit}\n  .honest{display:flex;gap:.7rem;background:#fff;border:1px solid var(--line);border-left:4px solid var(--green);border-radius:0 10px 10px 0;padding:12px 16px;margin:.9rem 0 0}\n  .honest b{color:var(--tx)}\n  .honest p{margin:.2rem 0 0;color:var(--mut);font-size:.93rem}\n  .nots{list-style:none;padding:0;margin:.8rem 0 0;display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:8px}\n  .nots li{display:flex;gap:.5rem;background:#FBF0ED;border:1px solid #f0d6cc;border-radius:8px;padding:.5rem .7rem;color:#6b3a2a;font-size:.9rem;margin:0}\n  .moat{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:10px;margin-top:.8rem}\n  .moat div{background:#EAF6EF;border:1px solid #BfE6D2;border-radius:10px;padding:10px 12px}\n  .moat h4{margin:0;font-size:.92rem;color:var(--green-d)}\n  .moat p{margin:.2rem 0 0;font-size:.86rem;color:#34453d}\n  .shot{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;text-align:center;background:#EEF3EC;border:1px dashed #BfD3C6;border-radius:10px;color:var(--mut);font-size:.82rem;padding:18px;margin-top:.8rem;min-height:96px}\n  .ghbtn{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:.5rem;background:#0C261F;color:#fff;font-weight:600;font-size:.9rem;text-decoration:none;border-radius:999px;padding:.5rem .95rem;margin-top:.9rem}\n  .cta{background:#EAF6EF;border:1px solid #BfE6D2;border-radius:12px;padding:16px 18px;margin:18px 0 6px;text-align:center}\n  .cta a{display:inline-block;background:var(--green);color:#04140D;font-weight:700;text-decoration:none;border-radius:999px;padding:.6rem 1.3rem;margin-top:.5rem}\n  table{border-collapse:collapse;margin-top:.8rem;font-size:.86rem;width:100%}\n  th,td{border:1px solid var(--line);padding:.35rem .55rem;text-align:left}\n  th{background:#EEF3EC}\n  .foot{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:.5rem;padding:16px 32px 26px;color:var(--mut);font-size:.85rem}\n  .foot a{text-decoration:none}\n  @media (max-width:680px){.nots,.moat,.shots{grid-template-columns:1fr}}\n  /* annotation-tool screenshots (no footage/people — pure UI) */\n  .shots{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:14px;margin-top:.9rem}\n  .shots figure{margin:0}\n  .shots img{width:100%;height:auto;display:block;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px}\n  .shots figcaption{margin-top:.4rem;font-size:.82rem;color:var(--mut)}\n  .sample{background:#EAF6EF;border:1px solid #BfE6D2;border-radius:10px;padding:11px 14px;margin:0 0 4px;font-size:.92rem;color:#34453d}\n  .sample a{font-weight:600}\n</style>\n</head>\n<body>\n<script type="module" src="/i18n.js"></script>\n<div class="sheet">\n  <div class="head">\n    <div aria-hidden="true">🌐</span><select data-langsw aria-label="Language" data-i18n="lang.label" data-i18n-attr="aria-label">\n          <option value="en" data-i18n="lang.en" lang="en">English</option>\n data-i18n-html="site.ownModel.heroTitle">Annotate your footage. <span class="hl">Train a model that's yours.</span></h1>\n    <p data-i18n-html="site.ownModel.heroLead">You can't train Gemini or GPT ### 88. assistant (2026-06-22T01:32:55.802Z)

// One-off authoring helper (NOT shipped): merge the workflow's kn/hi translations into the catalogs.
// Input: site/scripts/_translations.json = { kn: {<dotted key>: value, ...}, hi: {...} } where keys are
// RELATIVE to site.ownModel (e.g. "heroLead", "nav.home", "annotate.body"). requestAccess is intentionally
// absent (it was preserved from B5/B6, not re-translated) and is left untouched here.
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { JSDOM } from "jsdom";

const ROOT = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..");

// Expected key set = the en own-model leaves minus requestAccess (mirror of the workflow's enFlat).
const html = readFileSync(join(ROOT, "site", "public", "own-model.html"), "utf8");
const { document } = new JSDOM(html).window;
const flatten = (obj, p = "") => Object.entries(obj).reduce((a, [k, v]) => {
  const key = p ? `${p}.${k}` : k;
  if (v && typeof v === "object") Object.assign(a, flatten(v, key)); else a[key] = v; return a;
}, {});
const enObj = {};
for (const el of document.querySelectorAll("[data-i18n], [data-i18n-html]")) {
  if (el.getAttribute("data-i18n-attr")) continue;
  const hk = el.getAttribute("data-i18n-html");
  const key = hk || el.getAttribute("data-i18n");
  if (!key.startsWith("site.ownModel.")) continue;
  const parts = key.slice("site.ownModel.".length).split(".");
  let o = enObj; for (let i = 0; i < parts.length - 1; i++) o = o[parts[i]] = o[parts[i]] || {};
  o[parts[parts.length - 1]] = (hk ? el.innerHTML : el.textContent).trim();
}
const EXPECTED = Object.keys(flatten(enObj)).filter((k) => k !== "requestAccess");

const T = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, "site", "scripts", "_translations.json"), "utf8"));
let bad = 0;
for (const loc of ["kn", "hi"]) {
  const flat = T[loc];
  if (!flat) { console.error(`MISSING locale ${loc} in _translations.json`); bad++; continue; }
  const missing = EXPECTED.filter((k) => !(k in flat));
  const extra = Object.keys(flat).filter((k) => !EXPECTED.includes(k));
  if (missing.length) { console.error(`${loc} MISSING ${missing.length} keys:`, missing.join(", ")); bad++; }
  if (extra.length) console.error(`${loc} has ${extra.length} unexpected keys:`, extra.join(", "));
  const p = join(ROOT, "locales", loc, "common.json");
  const cat = JSON.parse(readFileSync(p, "utf8"));
  const own = cat.site.ownModel;
  for (const [dotted, val] of Object.entries(flat)) {
    if (!EXPECTED.includes(dotted)) continue; // never write a stray key (would break key-parity)
    const parts = dotted.split(".");
    let o = own; for (let i = 0; i < parts.length - 1; i++) o = o[parts[i]] = o[parts[i]] || {};
    o[parts[parts.length - 1]] = val;
  }
  writeFileSync(p, JSON.stringify(cat, null, 2) + "\n");
  console.log(`${loc}: wrote ${Object.keys(flat).length} translated keys (requestAccess preserved = "${own.requestAccess}")`);
}
if (bad) { console.error("FAILED: incomplete translations — fix _translations.json"); process.exit(1); }
console.log(`OK: ${EXPECTED.length} keys per locale applied.`);

1	// Content regression guards for the localization catalogs — the rules the adversarial translation
2	// audit (#77) established, pinned so the specific bugs can never silently return. This complements
3	// hygiene.test.ts (which proves key/placeholder PARITY) by asserting catalog CONTENT. Each block maps
4	// to a concrete #77 bug class; the mutation that reintroduces the bug fails here. Pure data assertions
5	// over repo-root /locales (D-CATALOG-HOME) — no render context needed.
6	import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
7	import { readFileSync, readdirSync } from "node:fs";
8	import { join } from "node:path";
9	
10	const LOCALES_DIR = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..", "..", "locales");
11	
12	type Flat = Record<string, string>;
13	function flatEntries(obj: Record<string, unknown>, prefix = ""): Flat {
14	  const out: Flat = {};
15	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(obj)) {
16	    const key = prefix ? `${prefix}.${k}` : k;
17	    if (v && typeof v === "object" && !Array.isArray(v)) Object.assign(out, flatEntries(v as Record<string, unknown>, key));
18	    else out[key] = String(v);
19	  }
20	  return out;
21	}
22	const load = (loc: string): Flat =>
23	  flatEntries(JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(LOCALES_DIR, loc, "common.json"), "utf8")) as Record<string, unknown>);
24	
25	const ALL_LOCALES = readdirSync(LOCALES_DIR, { withFileTypes: true })
26	  .filter((d) => d.isDirectory())
27	  .map((d) => d.name);
28	const en = load("en");
29	
30	// (1) Internal-token / debug-tag leaks — EVERY locale. The engine's internal `min_shot_count` config
31	// name leaked into footer.floorSomeRest (#77, kn even wrapped it in <code>). No developer/markup tag
32	// (code/kbd/samp/tt/var) or that token may reach any user-facing string.
33	describe("catalog content — no internal token / debug-tag leaks (every locale)", () => {
34	  const TOKEN = /min_shot_count/;
35	  const DEBUG_TAG = /<\/?(?:code|kbd|samp|tt|var)\b/i;
36	  for (const loc of ALL_LOCALES) {
37	    it(`${loc}: no 'min_shot_count' token or <code>/<kbd>/<samp>/<tt>/<var> tag`, () => {
38	      const offenders = Object.entries(load(loc))
39	        .filter(([, v]) => TOKEN.test(v) || DEBUG_TAG.test(v))
40	        .map(([k]) => k);
41	      expect(offenders).toEqual([]);
42	    });
43	  }
44	});
45	
46	// (2) Brand — never transliterated, and present (Latin) on EVERY key en uses it on. The positive rule
47	// is the real guard: self-maintaining (auto-covers future brand keys, incl. errors.* / ingest.hint that
48	// the old 3-key denylist missed) and it catches ANY transliteration spelling at once — a transliterated
49	// value simply won't contain Latin "KhelSutra". The Indic-spelling denylist is cheap defence-in-depth.
50	describe("catalog content — the brand stays Latin 'KhelSutra' everywhere en uses it", () => {
51	  const brandKeys = Object.keys(en).filter((k) => en[k].includes("KhelSutra"));
52	
53	  it("en actually carries the brand on several keys (so the guard is meaningful)", () => {
54	    expect(brandKeys.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(6);
55	  });
56	
57	  for (const loc of ["kn", "hi"] as const) {
58	    it(`${loc}: Latin "KhelSutra" is present on every key whose en value uses it`, () => {
59	      const e = load(loc);
60	      const missing = brandKeys.filter((k) => !(e[k] ?? "").includes("KhelSutra"));
61	      expect(missing).toEqual([]);
62	    });
63	  }
64	
65	  const TRANSLIT: Record<string, string> = { kn: "ಖೇಲ್‌ಸೂತ್ರ", hi: "खेलसूत्र" };
66	  for (const loc of ["kn", "hi"] as const) {
67	    it(`${loc}: the known Indic transliteration of the brand never appears`, () => {
68	      const offenders = Object.entries(load(loc))
69	        .filter(([, v]) => v.includes(TRANSLIT[loc]))
70	        .map(([k]) => k);
71	      expect(offenders).toEqual([]);
72	    });
73	  }
74	});
75	
76	// (3) Engine/server/bucket/backend jargon only where en uses it (kn/hi). Self-maintaining: a localized

78	// also uses the English word. en avoids these in body copy (uses the brand / "cloud-storage" / passive
79	// voice) EXCEPT the technical ingest.uriAriaLabel + uriPlaceholder; #77 leaked them into kn/hi elsewhere.

95	            ...TRANSLIT[loc][w].filter((t) => val.includes(t)),
96	          ];
97	          for (const term of present) if (!enUsesWord(w)) violations.push({ key, term, en: en[key] ?? "(absent)" });

99	      }
100	      expect(violations).toEqual([]);
101	    });
102	  }
103	});

=== parity.yml ===
# Live-host parity — the site runs on TWO hosts (Cloudflare Worker + the DO droplet's
# Node/SQLite twin) that must serve byte-identical pages. The unit suite proves the code
# works on each host; it CANNOT see that the two LIVE URLs have drifted (e.g. a merge
# auto-deployed Cloudflare but the droplet wasn't re-synced from git). This runs the
# existing scripts/parity-check.sh against both live URLs on a schedule — NOT on PRs, so
# it never blocks development; a failure surfaces real production drift to the owner.
name: live-parity

on:
  schedule:
    - cron: "17 6 * * *" # daily ~06:17 UTC
  workflow_dispatch: {} # and on demand

# Avoid overlapping runs if a manual dispatch races the cron.
concurrency:
  group: live-parity
  cancel-in-progress: false

jobs:
  parity:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - name: Compare the two live hosts byte-for-byte
        env:
          CF_URL: https://khelsutra.guru
          VM_URL: http://168.144.126.176:8787
        run: bash site/scripts/parity-check.sh
