### 135. assistant (2026-06-22T01:46:26.348Z)

// Records the localized own-model ("Annotate your footage / train your own model") page:
// desktop journey en -> Kannada -> scroll annotate/train/setup -> Hindi. Captures per-locale stills
// and asserts the console is clean + the page actually rendered Indic script (glyph proxy for the
// owner's real-device eyeball). Run against the worktree site server (URL env, default :8123).
import { chromium } from "playwright";
import { rename } from "node:fs/promises";
import { mkdirSync } from "node:fs";

const URL = process.env.URL || "http://localhost:8123";
const HOLD = 1500;
mkdirSync("rec", { recursive: true });
const browser = await chromium.launch();

const errors = [];
const ctx = await browser.newContext({
  viewport: { width: 1280, height: 900 },
  locale: "en-US",
  recordVideo: { dir: "rec", size: { width: 1280, height: 900 } },
});
const page = await ctx.newPage();
page.on("console", (m) => { if (m.type() === "error") errors.push(m.text()); });
page.on("pageerror", (e) => errors.push("pageerror: " + e.message));

await page.goto(URL + "/own-model", { waitUntil: "networkidle" });
await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
await page.screenshot({ path: "ownmodel-en.png", fullPage: true });

// Switch to Kannada via the compact header switcher, then walk the three sections.
await page.selectOption("select[data-langsw]", "kn");
await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
const knHero = await page.locator("h1").first().innerText();
await page.screenshot({ path: "ownmodel-kn-hero.png" });
for (const id of ["#annotate", "#train", "#setup"]) {
  await page.locator(id).scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
  await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
}
await page.screenshot({ path: "ownmodel-kn-full.png", fullPage: true });

// Switch to Hindi.
await page.evaluate(() => window.scrollTo(0, 0));
await page.selectOption("select[data-langsw]", "hi");
await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
const hiHero = await page.locator("h1").first().innerText();
await page.screenshot({ path: "ownmodel-hi-hero.png" });
await page.locator("#train").scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
await page.screenshot({ path: "ownmodel-hi-full.png", fullPage: true });

const v = page.video();
await ctx.close();
await rename(await v.path(), "khelsutra-ownmodel-i18n-desktop.webm");

const KN = /[ಀ-೿]/, HI = /[ऀ-ॿ]/;
console.log("kn hero:", JSON.stringify(knHero));
console.log("hi hero:", JSON.stringify(hiHero));
console.log("kn hero in Kannada script:", KN.test(knHero));
console.log("hi hero in Devanagari script:", HI.test(hiHero));
console.log("console errors:", errors.length ? errors : "NONE");
await browser.close();
console.log("wrote khelsutra-ownmodel-i18n-desktop.webm + ownmodel-{en,kn-hero,kn-full,hi-hero,hi-full}.png");

### 170. assistant (2026-06-22T01:53:23.800Z)

Done. The own-model kn/hi localization is **merged to master** ([#86](https://github.com/avidullu/khelsutra/pull/86), squash `17242a9`) with full A3-style diligence.

## What shipped
The developer "Annotate your footage / train your own model" page (`site/public/own-model.html`) — previously English-only on kn/hi except its switcher + request link — now renders **fully in-language**:

- **52 new `site.ownModel.*` keys** wired via `data-i18n`/`data-i18n-html` (hero, annotate/train/setup steps, the moat grid, both honest-framing fences, "what this is not", CTA, footer; inline `<b>/<i>/<a>/<code>/<span>` preserved).
- **Deliberately literal:** `<pre>`/CLI blocks, file paths, the `ending_reason` enum, brand/tool tokens (KhelSutra/VLC/rally-annotator/MIT/GPU/LLM), and the VLC UI button labels shown in the screenshots. Every honest caveat kept intact.
- **en authored byte-for-byte** (jsdom-extracted → the A3 drift guard validates it); es/da/id en-seeded; **kn/hi AI-drafted → 3-lens adversarial verify (accuracy/fluency/integrity) → revise** via a 10-agent workflow (16 kn + 10 hi findings folded). The shipped `requestAccess` translation was preserved.

## Verified (the pre-LGTM bar, run locally)
- **site `npm test`: 75 ✓** (incl. the byte-for-byte drift guard) · **web: 215 ✓** · typecheck clean · **coverage 93.4/88.1/85.9% ≥ floor (92/87/84), no regression** · build ✓.
- New tests: own-model body-wiring assertion + kn/hi in-script render check + a tag-multiset/literal-token integrity guard.
- **Real-browser pass** (kn + hi render with Noto glyphs, `<b>` bold preserved, code blocks literal, **zero console errors**); screencast `khelsutra-ownmodel-i18n-desktop.webm` + per-locale stills in `../khelsutra-screencast/`.
- **CI green on the PR: site ✓, web ✓, Cloudflare Workers build ✓.**

## Notable
- Built in an isolated worktree off `origin/master` — vindicated: the shared checkout churned to `deploy/kit-live-verified` *and* a sibling PR (#85) merged mid-session. I confirmed my branch carried only my 2 commits before the squash, so no sibling work was absorbed.
- One justified test-guard tweak: the web `catalog-invariants` `<code>`-leak rule (from #77) is **scoped** to allow inline code only on `site.ownModel.*` (the SPA-console leak guard stays intact everywhere else).

## Still open (unchanged — both human/owner gates)
The §9 DoD is **not** flipped to DONE and nothing is archived. The two standing gates remain: **native-speaker review** of the AI-drafted kn/hi, and your **font-glyph eyeball on a real device**. With #86, **no engineering gap remains** before those gates (access-form kn/hi is now the largest remaining English-on-kn/hi surface, but it's a separate, optional A3-style slice — not a DoD blocker). Handoff + doc updated accordingly.

### 176. assistant (2026-06-22T01:58:09.644Z)

I dug into the actual state before doing anything, and the request rests on a mismatch worth surfacing:

**What actually exists** (`/locales`): `en`, `kn`, `hi`, `es` (Spanish), `da` (**Danish**), `id` (Indonesian). There is **no Dutch (`nl`) or Thai (`th`) anywhere** — I grepped the whole repo. ("Danish" and "Dutch" are easy to swap.)

**Why the site dropdown is only en/kn/hi** — it's by design, not a bug:
- The **SPA console** strings *are* translated for es/da/id (e.g. es `"Elige las jugadas para tu reel"`).
- But the **marketing-site namespace** (`site.*`, 129 keys — nav/hero/record/capture/join/ownModel/reveal) is **100% English-seeded** for es/da/id (0 of 129 translated). So if I just added them to the dropdown, picking "Español" would render the entire site in **English under a Spanish label** — i.e. it wouldn't "work" in the sense you mean.

So "add them and make sure it works" really means: **translate the full marketing site into each added language** (the same A3/own-model-style AI-draft → adversarial-verify pass), then wire the dropdown. That's a real, sizable slice — and which languages to invest in is a product call, so I want to confirm rather than guess.

### 220. assistant (2026-06-22T03:25:09.236Z)

// One-off authoring helper (NOT shipped): merge the workflow's es/da/id translations into the catalogs.
// Input: site/scripts/_esdaid_translations.json = { es:{"site.<dotted>":value,...}, da:{...}, id:{...} }.
// Target set = the site.* keys kn translated (so es/da/id reach the same coverage parity); the 14
// site.join.* form keys stay English in EVERY locale (A6 deferred) and are intentionally untouched.
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";

const ROOT = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..");
const load = (l) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, "locales", l, "common.json"), "utf8"));
const flat = (o, p = "") => Object.entries(o).reduce((a, [k, v]) => {
  const key = p ? `${p}.${k}` : k;
  if (v && typeof v === "object" && !Array.isArray(v)) Object.assign(a, flat(v, key)); else a[key] = String(v);
  return a;
}, {});

const en = load("en"), kn = load("kn");
const ef = flat(en.site, "site"), kf = flat(kn.site, "site");
const EXPECTED = Object.keys(ef).filter((k) => kf[k] !== ef[k]); // 115 keys kn translated

const T = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, "site", "scripts", "_esdaid_translations.json"), "utf8"));
let bad = 0;
for (const loc of ["es", "da", "id"]) {
  const flatT = T[loc];
  if (!flatT) { console.error(`MISSING locale ${loc}`); bad++; continue; }
  const missing = EXPECTED.filter((k) => !(k in flatT));
  const extra = Object.keys(flatT).filter((k) => !EXPECTED.includes(k));
  if (missing.length) { console.error(`${loc} MISSING ${missing.length}:`, missing.join(", ")); bad++; }
  if (extra.length) console.error(`${loc} ${extra.length} unexpected keys (ignored):`, extra.join(", "));
  const cat = load(loc);
  for (const dotted of EXPECTED) {
    if (!(dotted in flatT)) continue;
    const parts = dotted.split(".");
    let o = cat; for (let i = 0; i < parts.length - 1; i++) o = o[parts[i]] = o[parts[i]] || {};
    o[parts[parts.length - 1]] = flatT[dotted];
  }
  writeFileSync(join(ROOT, "locales", loc, "common.json"), JSON.stringify(cat, null, 2) + "\n");
  console.log(`${loc}: applied ${EXPECTED.filter((k) => k in flatT).length}/${EXPECTED.length} keys`);
}
if (bad) { console.error("FAILED: incomplete translations"); process.exit(1); }
console.log(`OK: ${EXPECTED.length} target keys per locale (site.join.* left English in all locales).`);

### 249. assistant (2026-06-22T03:33:05.959Z)

// Records the marketing site reaching parity with the SPA's 6 languages: the home page driven
// through the compact switcher en -> Spanish -> Danish -> Indonesian, plus the capture + own-model
// pages in Spanish. Captures per-locale stills and asserts the console is clean + the hero copy
// actually changed per language. Run against the worktree site server (URL env, default :8124).
import { chromium } from "playwright";
import { rename } from "node:fs/promises";
import { mkdirSync } from "node:fs";

const URL = process.env.URL || "http://localhost:8124";
const HOLD = 1300;
mkdirSync("rec", { recursive: true });
const browser = await chromium.launch();

const errors = [];
const ctx = await browser.newContext({
  viewport: { width: 1280, height: 900 },
  locale: "en-US",
  recordVideo: { dir: "rec", size: { width: 1280, height: 900 } },
});
const page = await ctx.newPage();
page.on("console", (m) => { if (m.type() === "error") errors.push(m.text()); });
page.on("pageerror", (e) => errors.push("pageerror: " + e.message));

const heroOf = () => page.locator("h1").first().innerText();
const heros = {};

await page.goto(URL + "/", { waitUntil: "networkidle" });
await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
heros.en = await heroOf();
await page.screenshot({ path: "site-en-home.png" });

for (const loc of ["es", "da", "id"]) {
  await page.evaluate(() => window.scrollTo(0, 0));
  await page.selectOption("select[data-langsw]", loc);
  await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
  heros[loc] = await heroOf();
  await page.screenshot({ path: `site-${loc}-home.png` });
  // scroll the recording-education section into view (the highest-value localized marketing block)
  await page.locator("#record").scrollIntoViewIfNeeded().catch(() => {});
  await page.waitForTimeout(700);
  await page.screenshot({ path: `site-${loc}-record.png`, fullPage: false });
}

// capture + own-model pages in Spanish (es persisted from the switch above)
await page.goto(URL + "/capture", { waitUntil: "networkidle" });
await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
await page.screenshot({ path: "site-es-capture.png", fullPage: true });
await page.goto(URL + "/own-model", { waitUntil: "networkidle" });
await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
await page.screenshot({ path: "site-es-ownmodel.png", fullPage: true });

const v = page.video();
await ctx.close();
await rename(await v.path(), "khelsutra-site-esdaid-desktop.webm");

console.log("heroes:");
for (const [k, val] of Object.entries(heros)) console.log(" ", k, JSON.stringify(val));
const distinct = new Set(Object.values(heros)).size;
console.log("all 4 heroes distinct (en/es/da/id):", distinct === 4);
console.log("console errors:", errors.length ? errors : "NONE");
await browser.close();
console.log("wrote khelsutra-site-esdaid-desktop.webm + site-{en,es,da,id}-home.png + es capture/own-model");

Done — **es/da/id are now in the site dropdown and fully working**, merged to master via [#87](https://github.com/avidullu/khelsutra/pull/87) (squash `cf59fbf`).

## First, the correction that shaped this
There's **no Dutch or Thai** anywhere in the repo — the existing extra locales are `es` (Spanish), `da` (**Danish**, not Dutch), `id` (Indonesian). They were translated in the SPA console but their *marketing-site* copy was 100% English-seeded, which is why the dropdown was intentionally en/kn/hi. "Make it work" therefore meant translating the whole site into each — which is what shipped.

## What shipped
- **Dropdown + negotiation:** es/da/id `<option>`s on all 3 pages; shim `SUPPORTED` + `server.js` `SITE_LOCALES` now list all 6 → a Spanish/Danish/Indonesian browser auto-renders the site in-language (`<html lang>` + `content-language`).
- **Translation:** the **115 `site.*` keys** (nav, hero, recording-education, capture + diagram, own-model, reveal) into es/da/id — **AI-drafted → 3-lens adversarial verify → revise** (11 es + 10 da + 14 id findings folded), reusing each locale's shipped app vocabulary (rally → es *jugada*, da *duel*, id *reli*). HTML tags + literal tokens preserved.
- **Parity, not over-reach:** the access form (`site.join.*`) stays English in es/da/id too, exactly as for kn/hi (A6 deferred form translation for all non-en).

## Verified
- **site 77 ✓ · web 215 ✓** · typecheck clean · coverage 93.4% (≥ floor) · build ✓ · **CI green on the PR (site + web + Cloudflare)**.
- Real-browser: home heroes render distinct & correct (*Cada jugada, indexada* / *Hver duel, indekseret* / *Setiap reli, terindeks*), the Indonesian capture page + camera diagram render, no i18n console errors (only the pre-existing Cloudflare analytics beacon, which can't reach its endpoint from localhost). Screencast `khelsutra-site-esdaid-desktop.webm` + per-locale stills saved.

## Honest notes
- **es/da/id are AI-drafted → native-review-pending**, same standing pre-launch gate as kn/hi.
- I found one pre-existing gap: a few **hardcoded hero-mockup chips** in `index.html` (*See it in action*, *Auto-index rallies*, *Download your reel*) aren't `data-i18n`-wired, so they show English in **every** non-en locale **including kn/hi** today — not something this PR introduced. I left it out of scope (it'd touch all 6 languages) and **flagged it as a follow-up chip** for you, plus recorded it in the handoff.
- Worked in an isolated worktree off `origin/master` — again vindicated: a sibling session's worktree (`khelsutra-p2c`) was active concurrently. Branch carried only my 2 commits before the squash; cleaned up after.

1	// i18n scaffold (tracker B1) — the shared localization foundation for KhelSutra Studio.
2	//
3	// Per docs/FRIENDLY_AND_MULTILINGUAL.md + the engine's I18N_PLAN.md:
4	//   - keys, never prose (t("app.title"), enforced later by a no-bare-strings lint, B2);
5	//   - ICU MessageFormat for interpolation/plurals (Kannada/Hindi plural rules live in the catalog,
6	//     not the code — replaces the SPA's hand-rolled `count > 1 ? "s" : ""`);
7	//   - one negotiation order shared with the marketing site (B3);
8	//   - always-fallback to `en` so a missing translation degrades to English, never a raw key.
9	import i18n from "i18next";
10	import { initReactI18next } from "react-i18next";
11	import LanguageDetector from "i18next-browser-languagedetector";
12	import ICU from "i18next-icu";
13	// Catalogs are HOISTED to repo-root /locales (D-CATALOG-HOME) — the single source of truth shared
14	// by this SPA and the marketing site's t() shim (tracker B3). Vite resolves the workspace root via
15	// the repo `.git` marker, so importing the parent-dir /locales works in dev, build, and vitest.
16	import enCommon from "../../../locales/en/common.json";
17	import knCommon from "../../../locales/kn/common.json";
18	import hiCommon from "../../../locales/hi/common.json";
19	import esCommon from "../../../locales/es/common.json";
20	import daCommon from "../../../locales/da/common.json";
21	import idCommon from "../../../locales/id/common.json";
22	
23	// Supported locales: en/kn/hi foundation (FRIENDLY D6) + MULTILINGUAL_EXPANSION Phase A (es/da/id,
24	// Latin, catalog-only). Anything else falls back to `en`.
25	export const SUPPORTED_LOCALES = ["en", "kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"] as const;
26	export type Locale = (typeof SUPPORTED_LOCALES)[number];
27	export const DEFAULT_LOCALE: Locale = "en";
28	
29	// ONE persisted-preference key, shared with the marketing site's t() shim (D9 / tracker B3), so a
30	// visitor's language choice carries across both surfaces.
31	export const LANG_STORAGE_KEY = "khelsutra.lang";
32	
33	// Catalogs live in repo-root /locales (D-CATALOG-HOME), consumed by both surfaces. `kn` + `hi`
34	// shipped (B5/B6, AI-drafted → native review pending). A missing key in any locale still falls back
35	// to `en` — the by-design invariant.
36	const resources = {
37	  en: { common: enCommon },
38	  kn: { common: knCommon },
39	  hi: { common: hiCommon },
40	  es: { common: esCommon },
41	  da: { common: daCommon },
42	  id: { common: idCommon },
43	} as const;
44	
45	void i18n
46	  .use(ICU)
47	  .use(LanguageDetector)
48	  .use(initReactI18next)
49	  .init({
50	    resources,
51	    fallbackLng: DEFAULT_LOCALE,
52	    supportedLngs: [...SUPPORTED_LOCALES],
53	    nonExplicitSupportedLngs: true, // en-IN -> en
54	    defaultNS: "common",
55	    ns: ["common"],
56	    interpolation: { escapeValue: false }, // React already escapes
57	    returnNull: false,
58	    // Negotiation order (I18N_PLAN §2.3): persisted pref -> ?lang= -> browser -> en.
59	    detection: {
60	      order: ["localStorage", "querystring", "navigator"],
61	      lookupLocalStorage: LANG_STORAGE_KEY,
62	      lookupQuerystring: "lang",
63	      caches: ["localStorage"],
64	    },
65	    react: { useSuspense: false },
66	  });
67	
68	// Keep <html lang> in sync with the active locale (screen readers + SEO use it). Reflects the
69	// resolved content language, so an `en` fallback for an absent locale still reads as `en`.
70	function syncDocumentLang(lng: string) {
71	  if (typeof document !== "undefined") {
72	    document.documentElement.lang = (i18n.resolvedLanguage || lng || DEFAULT_LOCALE).split("-")[0];
73	  }
74	}
75	i18n.on("languageChanged", syncDocumentLang);
76	syncDocumentLang(i18n.language ?? DEFAULT_LOCALE);
77	
78	export default i18n;
79

### 297. assistant (2026-06-22T03:51:14.519Z)

// Proves the §6.5 promise on the SITE: a browser whose OS/locale is X auto-RENDERS content in X,
// with NO dropdown interaction. For each locale we open a fresh context with that `locale` (which sets
// navigator.language + Accept-Language), load the home page, and read the rendered hero + <html lang>.
import { chromium } from "playwright";
const URL = process.env.URL || "http://localhost:8131";
const browser = await chromium.launch();

// expected hero opening per locale (first few words) to prove CONTENT, not just the lang attribute
const EXPECT = {
  en: "Every rally", kn: "ಪ್ರತಿ", hi: "हर", es: "Cada jugada", da: "Hver duel", id: "Setiap reli",
  fr: "Every rally", // unsupported -> must fall back to English content
};
let allOk = true;
for (const loc of ["en", "kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id", "fr"]) {
  const playwrightLocale = { en: "en-US", kn: "kn-IN", hi: "hi-IN", es: "es-MX", da: "da-DK", id: "id-ID", fr: "fr-FR" }[loc];
  const ctx = await browser.newContext({ locale: playwrightLocale });
  const page = await ctx.newPage();
  const errs = [];
  page.on("pageerror", (e) => errs.push(e.message));
  await page.goto(URL + "/", { waitUntil: "networkidle" });
  await page.waitForTimeout(900); // let the client shim negotiate + apply
  const hero = (await page.locator("h1").first().innerText()).replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  const htmlLang = await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.lang);
  const want = loc === "fr" ? "en" : loc;
  const ok = htmlLang === want && hero.startsWith(EXPECT[loc]) && (errs.length === 0);
  if (!ok) allOk = false;
  console.log(`${ok ? "✓" : "✗"} browser=${playwrightLocale} -> html.lang=${htmlLang} (want ${want}) | hero="${hero.slice(0, 42)}…"${errs.length ? " ERRORS:" + errs.join(";") : ""}`);
  await ctx.close();
}
await browser.close();
console.log(allOk ? "\nALL PASS: browser/OS language auto-renders the right content (site, client shim, no dropdown)." : "\nFAILURES above.");

## Yes — all 6 locales auto-render from the browser/OS language, on both surfaces

I just proved it end-to-end rather than assuming it. A browser whose OS/language is set to each locale, with **no dropdown interaction**, auto-renders that language:

| Browser/OS locale | Site renders | ✓ |
|---|---|---|
| `kn-IN` | ಪ್ರತಿ ರ್ಯಾಲಿಯೂ ಸೂಚಿಯಲ್ಲಿ… (Kannada) | ✓ |
| `hi-IN` | हर रैली, सूची में… (Hindi) | ✓ |
| `es-MX` | Cada jugada, indexada… (Spanish) | ✓ |
| `da-DK` | Hver duel, indekseret… (Danish) | ✓ |
| `id-ID` | Setiap reli, terindeks… (Indonesian) | ✓ |
| `en-US` | Every rally, indexed… (English) | ✓ |
| `fr-FR` (unsupported) | English (correct fallback) | ✓ |

**How it works (both surfaces, same contract):** negotiation order is *persisted pref → `?lang=` → browser/`Accept-Language` → en*.
- **Site** (`server.js` + `i18n.js` shim): now lists all 6. I confirmed live across all 3 pages — server stamps `<html lang>`/`content-language` from `Accept-Language`, and the client shim applies the translated content from `navigator.languages`. Region subtags normalize (`es-MX`→es, `kn-IN`→kn); unsupported (fr/ta)→en.
- **SPA** (`web/`, react-i18next + LanguageDetector): `supportedLngs` = all 6 with `nonExplicitSupportedLngs: true` (so `es-419`→es etc.), detecting from navigator. All 6 detection tests pass (kn-IN, hi-IN, es-ES, da-DK, id-ID, en).

## Three honest caveats on "natively rendered"

1. **Kn/Hi glyphs** still need your **real-device eyeball** — the standing font gate. Noto is bundled (and renders fine on my machine + CI containers don't render fonts), but that's your DoD check. es/da/id are Latin — no font concern.
2. **Translation quality** is AI-drafted → **native-review-pending** for all 5 non-English locales. Auto-*selection* and *rendering* are proven; *wording* is the human gate.
3. **Not 100% of the copy is localized** — two pre-existing English islands remain in **every** non-en locale (incl. kn/hi): the hardcoded hero-mockup chips (now being fixed via the spawned chip) and the Request-Access form (`site.join.*`, A6-deferred). So an es/kn/… browser auto-renders the language, but those two specific blocks still show English until those slices land.

So: **auto-negotiation is complete and working for all 6 on both surfaces** — what's left before "fully native" is the two human gates plus those two known English islands.

405:        <p style="color:var(--mut);font-size:1.05rem" data-i18n-html="site.diff.p2">A single recording of a busy, multi-court hall is exactly where ordinary video tools choke. Pulling the <b style="color:var(--tx)">real</b> rallies out of busy, multi-court footage is the hard problem KhelSutra is built for. It already works very well on clean, single-court recordings — and the multi-court case is what we're sharpening now.</p>
420:      <h2 data-i18n="site.sharper.title">It gets sharper from real academy footage</h2>
421:      <p data-i18n="site.sharper.intro">The footage real academies contribute — especially the busy multi-court halls — is what makes KhelSutra better over time. Early users aren't just customers; they're co-builders.</p>
427:        <p data-i18n="site.sharper.improve.body">When you choose to share footage and coaches confirm what it found, that growing labelled library is what sharpens future versions — rather than staying frozen.</p>
432:        <p data-i18n="site.sharper.data.body">We never use your video to improve the tool without your clear, explicit opt-in. We never publish your footage. You decide what, if anything, you share.</p>
437:        <p data-i18n="site.sharper.minors.body">Footage involving minors is never used to improve the tool and is never published — full stop.</p>
442:      <p data-i18n="site.sharper.honestBody">Today the tool gives you rally detection, reels, and summaries. It does not retrain itself as you use it, and there's no per-person tracking. 517:      <details><summary data-i18n="site.faq.q5.q">Does it work for doubles and tournaments?</summary><p data-i18n="site.faq.q5.a">It finds rallies in doubles too. ### 327. assistant (2026-06-22T15:57:55.386Z)

// One-off authoring helper (NOT shipped): (1) extract site.footer.* en values byte-for-byte from the
// wired index.html, (2) seed them into all 6 catalogs (en authoritative; non-en seeded = en until the
// workflow translates), (3) emit the translation payload for site.join.* (already wired) + site.footer.*.
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { JSDOM } from "jsdom";

const ROOT = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..");
const load = (l) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, "locales", l, "common.json"), "utf8"));
const html = readFileSync(join(ROOT, "site", "public", "index.html"), "utf8");
const { document } = new JSDOM(html).window;

// 1) extract site.footer.* (textContent — these are plain strings, no inline markup)
const footer = {};
for (const el of document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n^="site.footer."]')) {
  const key = el.getAttribute("data-i18n").slice("site.footer.".length);
  footer[key] = el.textContent.trim();
}
console.error("extracted site.footer.* keys:", Object.keys(footer).join(", "));

// 2) seed into every catalog (en = authoritative; non-en seeded with en for now -> key-parity green)
for (const loc of ["en", "kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"]) {
  const cat = load(loc);
  cat.site.footer = cat.site.footer || {};
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(footer)) {
    if (loc === "en" || !(k in cat.site.footer)) cat.site.footer[k] = v; // don't clobber an existing translation
  }
  writeFileSync(join(ROOT, "locales", loc, "common.json"), JSON.stringify(cat, null, 2) + "\n");
}
console.error("seeded site.footer.* into all 6 catalogs");

// 3) build the payload: en values for the keys to translate (join 14 + footer 6) + per-locale console ref
const en = load("en");
const enTargets = {};
for (const k of Object.keys(en.site.join)) enTargets[`site.join.${k}`] = en.site.join[k];
for (const k of Object.keys(footer)) enTargets[`site.footer.${k}`] = footer[k];
const refOf = (c) => ({
  app: c.app, query_support: c.query.support, grid_rally: c.grid.rally,
  ingest_title: c.ingest.title, result_ready: c.result.ready, lang: c.lang,
  // existing site translations in this locale for register (nav + a hero line + the GPU-free reassurance area)
  site_nav: c.site.nav, site_hero_eyebrow: c.site.hero.eyebrow, site_record_intro: c.site.record.intro,
});
const payload = { enTargets, ref: { kn: refOf(load("kn")), hi: refOf(load("hi")), es: refOf(load("es")), da: refOf(load("da")), id: refOf(load("id")) } };
writeFileSync(join(ROOT, "site", "scripts", "_payload.json"), JSON.stringify(payload));
console.error("payload keys:", Object.keys(enTargets).length, "| bytes:", JSON.stringify(payload).length);
process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(footer, null, 2) + "\n");

### 337. assistant (2026-06-22T15:59:56.800Z)

// One-off authoring helper (NOT shipped): merge the workflow's join+footer translations into the
// kn/hi/es/da/id catalogs. Input: site/scripts/_jf.json = { kn:{"site.<dotted>":v,...}, hi, es, da, id }.
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
const ROOT = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..");
const load = (l) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, "locales", l, "common.json"), "utf8"));
const en = load("en");
const EXPECTED = [
  ...Object.keys(en.site.join).map((k) => `site.join.${k}`),
  ...Object.keys(en.site.footer).map((k) => `site.footer.${k}`),
];
const T = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, "site", "scripts", "_jf.json"), "utf8"));
let bad = 0;
for (const loc of ["kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"]) {
  const f = T[loc];
  if (!f) { console.error(`MISSING ${loc}`); bad++; continue; }
  const missing = EXPECTED.filter((k) => !(k in f));
  if (missing.length) { console.error(`${loc} MISSING:`, missing.join(", ")); bad++; }
  const cat = load(loc);
  for (const dotted of EXPECTED) {
    if (!(dotted in f)) continue;
    const parts = dotted.split(".");
    let o = cat; for (let i = 0; i < parts.length - 1; i++) o = o[parts[i]] = o[parts[i]] || {};
    o[parts[parts.length - 1]] = f[dotted];
  }
  writeFileSync(join(ROOT, "locales", loc, "common.json"), JSON.stringify(cat, null, 2) + "\n");
  console.log(`${loc}: applied ${EXPECTED.filter((k) => k in f).length}/${EXPECTED.length}`);
}
if (bad) process.exit(1);
console.log("OK");

### 339. user (2026-06-22T16:00:00.078Z)

1	// Browser-layout regression guard (NOT part of `npm test` — it needs a real browser engine, so it
2	// runs as a separate `npm run test:layout` job in CI). The rest of the suite runs in jsdom, which
3	// has NO layout engine (getBoundingClientRect() is all zeros) and therefore cannot see overflow,
4	// wrapping, or clipping. This drives a headless Chromium over the REAL pages at a spread of
5	// viewport widths x all 6 locales and asserts the header never distorts — the exact class of bug
6	// that bit the responsive nav three times (links wrapping onto 3 lines + overlapping the brand, and
7	// the "Request access" CTA getting clipped off the right edge in wide-autonym locales like id).
8	//
9	// Assertions are layout INVARIANTS (inequalities with generous tolerances), not pixel screenshots —
10	// so they're robust across OS/font environments and need no baselines, while still catching the
11	// gross distortions that actually happened (50-75px of overflow / a 3-line nav).
12	import { test, before, after } from "node:test";
13	import assert from "node:assert/strict";
14	import { createServer } from "node:http";
15	import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
16	import { join, extname } from "node:path";
17	import { chromium } from "playwright";
18	
19	const PUBLIC = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "public");
20	const TYPES = {
21	  ".html": "text/html; charset=utf-8", ".js": "text/javascript", ".mjs": "text/javascript",
22	  ".json": "application/json", ".css": "text/css", ".png": "image/png", ".jpg": "image/jpeg",
23	  ".svg": "image/svg+xml", ".webp": "image/webp", ".webm": "video/webm", ".mp4": "video/mp4",
24	  ".woff2": "font/woff2", ".woff": "font/woff", ".ico": "image/x-icon",
25	};
26	
27	let server, browser, baseURL;
28	
29	before(async () => {
30	  // Minimal static server for site/public (clean-URL fallback: /capture -> capture.html).
31	  server = createServer(async (req, res) => {
32	    try {
33	      let p = decodeURIComponent(req.url.split("?")[0]);
34	      if (p === "/") p = "/index.html";
35	      let data, ext = extname(p);
36	      try { data = await readFile(join(PUBLIC, p)); }
37	      catch { try { data = await readFile(join(PUBLIC, p + ".html")); ext = ".html"; } catch { res.writeHead(404); return res.end("not found"); } }
38	      res.writeHead(200, { "content-type": TYPES[ext] || "application/octet-stream", "cache-control": "no-store" });
39	      res.end(data);
40	    } catch (e) { res.writeHead(500); res.end(String(e)); }
41	  });
42	  await new Promise((r) => server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", r));
43	  baseURL = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`;
44	  browser = await chromium.launch();
45	});
46	
47	after(async () => {
48	  await browser?.close();
49	  await new Promise((r) => server?.close(r));
50	});
51	
52	const PAGES = ["/index.html", "/capture.html", "/own-model.html"];
53	const LOCALES = ["en", "kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"]; // id (Bahasa Indonesia) is the widest-autonym worst case
54	const WIDTHS = [1280, 1100, 1000, 900, 768, 375]; // desktop + the old 860-1080 "dead zone" + tablet + phone
55	
56	// Tolerances tuned to ignore sub-pixel / font-rounding noise while catching the gross distortions that
57	// actually occurred (the incidents were 50-75px). The nav-links height check is the strongest signal:
58	// hidden = 0px, one line ~= 24px, the wrapped-and-overlapping bug was ~75px.
59	const TOL = { overflow: 16, ctaClip: 8, navLinksMaxHeight: 44, docOverflow: 16 };
60	
61	test("header never overflows / wraps over the brand / clips the CTA (pages x 6 locales x widths)", async () => {
62	  const failures = [];
63	  for (const path of PAGES) {
64	    for (const loc of LOCALES) {
65	      const page = await browser.newPage();
66	      try {
67	        await page.goto(`${baseURL}${path}?lang=${loc}`, { waitUntil: "load" });
68	        // Wait for the shim to apply the locale (so nav text is translated -> real widths) and for
69	        // webfonts to settle (text width is the whole point). en may already be the default.
70	        await page.waitForFunction((l) => document.querySelector("[data-langsw]")?.value === l, loc, { timeout: 6000 }).catch(() => {});
71	        await page.evaluate(() => (document.fonts ? document.fonts.ready.then(() => true) : true));
72	        for (const width of WIDTHS) {
73	          await page.setViewportSize({ width, height: 900 });
74	          await page.waitForTimeout(70); // let the reflow settle
75	          const m = await page.evaluate(() => {
76	            const q = (s) => document.querySelector(s);
77	            const nav = q(".nav"), links = q(".nav-links"), de = document.documentElement;
78	            const cta = q(".nav-right .btn-dark") || q(".nav-right .btn");
79	            return {
80	              navOverflow: nav ? nav.scrollWidth - nav.clientWidth : 0,
81	              navLinksHeight: links ? Math.round(links.getBoundingClientRect().height) : 0,
82	              ctaRight: cta ? Math.round(cta.getBoundingClientRect().right) : 0,
83	              docOverflow: de.scrollWidth - de.clientWidth,
84	            };
85	          });
86	          const at = `${path} [${loc}] @${width}px`;
87	          if (m.navOverflow > TOL.overflow) failures.push(`${at}: nav overflows horizontally by ${m.navOverflow}px`);
88	          if (m.navLinksHeight > TOL.navLinksMaxHeight) failures.push(`${at}: nav-links is ${m.navLinksHeight}px tall — wrapped onto multiple lines / overlapping the brand`);
89	          if (m.ctaRight && m.ctaRight > width + TOL.ctaClip) failures.push(`${at}: CTA right edge ${m.ctaRight}px is clipped past the ${width}px viewport`);
90	          if (m.docOverflow > TOL.docOverflow) failures.push(`${at}: page has ${m.docOverflow}px of horizontal overflow`);
91	        }
92	      } finally {
93	        await page.close();
94	      }
95	    }
96	  }
97	  assert.equal(failures.length, 0, failures.length ? `header layout distortions:\n  - ${failures.join("\n  - ")}\n` : "");
98	});
99	
100	// Companion to the no-overflow guard: prove the auto-collapse doesn't OVER-fire (a regression that
101	// hid the links for everyone would pass the overflow check vacuously). en has the shortest labels and
102	// the most headroom, so on desktop it must always keep its nav links visible.
103	test("a locale that fits keeps its nav links (auto-collapse doesn't over-fire) — index.html [en]", async () => {
104	  const page = await browser.newPage();
105	  try {
106	    await page.goto(`${baseURL}/index.html?lang=en`, { waitUntil: "load" });
107	    await page.evaluate(() => (document.fonts ? document.fonts.ready.then(() => true) : true));
108	    for (const width of [1280, 1200, 1100]) {
109	      await page.setViewportSize({ width, height: 900 });
110	      await page.waitForTimeout(80); // let fitNav settle after the resize
111	      const visible = await page.evaluate(() => {
112	        const l = document.querySelector(".nav-links");
113	        return !!l && getComputedStyle(l).display !== "none" && l.getBoundingClientRect().height > 0;
114	      });
115	      assert.ok(visible, `en nav links should stay visible at ${width}px (they fit) — auto-collapse over-fired`);
116	    }
117	  } finally {
118	    await page.close();
119	  }
120	});
121

### 353. assistant (2026-06-22T16:04:56.904Z)

// Cross-locale RENDER guard (separate `npm run test:render` job — needs a real browser, like the
// layout guard). jsdom proves the catalogs + static HTML defaults are consistent; it CANNOT prove the
// client shim (public/i18n.js) actually applies each locale correctly in a live DOM. This drives a
// headless Chromium over every localized page × all 6 locales and asserts, for each:
//   1. <html lang> resolves to the negotiated locale;
//   2. NO console / page errors;
//   3. EVERY data-i18n[-html] element's rendered text equals what the shim should produce for that
//      locale (active-locale value, en-fallback) — so a raw key, a missing/typo'd key, a stale render,
//      or a shim regression is caught on the REAL page, in EVERY language (not just en);
//   4. no visible raw key / un-interpolated {placeholder} / "undefined";
//   5. kn/hi pages actually contain their script (the translation + glyph path is live).
// This is the anti-regression net for "render every locale page and flag inconsistencies".
import { test, before, after } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { createServer } from "node:http";
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { join, extname } from "node:path";
import { chromium } from "playwright";

const PUBLIC = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "public");
const TYPES = {
  ".html": "text/html; charset=utf-8", ".js": "text/javascript", ".mjs": "text/javascript",
  ".json": "application/json; charset=utf-8", ".css": "text/css", ".png": "image/png", ".jpg": "image/jpeg",
  ".svg": "image/svg+xml", ".webp": "image/webp", ".webm": "video/webm", ".mp4": "video/mp4",
  ".woff2": "font/woff2", ".woff": "font/woff", ".ico": "image/x-icon",
};
let server, browser, baseURL;

before(async () => {
  server = createServer(async (req, res) => {
    try {
      let p = decodeURIComponent(req.url.split("?")[0]);
      if (p === "/") p = "/index.html";
      let data, ext = extname(p);
      try { data = await readFile(join(PUBLIC, p)); }
      catch { try { data = await readFile(join(PUBLIC, p + ".html")); ext = ".html"; } catch { res.writeHead(404); return res.end("not found"); } }
      res.writeHead(200, { "content-type": TYPES[ext] || "application/octet-stream", "cache-control": "no-store" });
      res.end(data);
    } catch (e) { res.writeHead(500); res.end(String(e)); }
  });
  await new Promise((r) => server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", r));
  baseURL = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`;
  browser = await chromium.launch();
});
after(async () => { await browser?.close(); await new Promise((r) => server?.close(r)); });

const PAGES = ["/index.html", "/capture.html", "/own-model.html"];
const LOCALES = ["en", "kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"];
const SCRIPT = { kn: /[ಀ-೿]/, hi: /[ऀ-ॿ]/ };
// namespaces that should never appear verbatim as visible text (= a raw, unresolved i18n key)
const KEY_RE = /^(site|app|query|grid|footer|ingest|result|errors|tap|selection|bulk|mode|lang|build|wizard)\.[a-zA-Z0-9.]+$/;

test("every localized page renders each locale correctly (DOM == catalog, no raw keys, right lang, no errors)", async () => {
  const failures = [];
  for (const path of PAGES) {
    for (const loc of LOCALES) {
      const ctx = await browser.newContext(); // isolated storage so ?lang wins cleanly
      const page = await ctx.newPage();
      const errors = [];
      page.on("console", (m) => { if (m.type() === "error") errors.push(m.text()); });
      page.on("pageerror", (e) => errors.push("pageerror: " + e.message));
      try {
        await page.goto(`${baseURL}${path}?lang=${loc}`, { waitUntil: "load" });
        await page.waitForFunction((l) => document.querySelector("[data-langsw]")?.value === l, loc, { timeout: 6000 }).catch(() => {});
        await page.evaluate(() => (document.fonts ? document.fonts.ready.then(() => true) : true));
        const at = `${path} [${loc}]`;

        const realErrors = errors.filter((e) => !/favicon/i.test(e));
        if (realErrors.length) failures.push(`${at}: console/page errors: ${realErrors.join(" | ")}`);

        const r = await page.evaluate(async ({ loc, KEY_SRC }) => {
          const KEY = new RegExp(KEY_SRC);
          const fetchCat = async (l) => { try { const res = await fetch(`/locales/${l}/common.json`); return res.ok ? await res.json() : {}; } catch { return {}; } };
          const active = await fetchCat(loc), en = await fetchCat("en");
          const dig = (o, k) => k.split(".").reduce((a, c) => (a && typeof a === "object" ? a[c] : undefined), o);
          const norm = (html) => { const d = document.createElement("div"); d.innerHTML = html; return d.innerHTML; };
          const out = { htmlLang: document.documentElement.lang, mismatches: [], rawKeys: [], empties: [] };
          for (const el of document.querySelectorAll("[data-i18n], [data-i18n-html]")) {
            if (el.getAttribute("data-i18n-attr")) continue; // attribute defaults, not visible text
            const htmlKey = el.getAttribute("data-i18n-html");
            const key = htmlKey || el.getAttribute("data-i18n");
            let expected = dig(active, key); if (expected == null) expected = dig(en, key);
            if (expected == null) continue; // missing everywhere -> shim keeps the DOM default; not its bug
            const got = htmlKey ? el.innerHTML : el.textContent.trim();
            const want = htmlKey ? norm(expected) : String(expected).trim();
            if (got !== want) out.mismatches.push(`${key}: DOM=${JSON.stringify(got.slice(0, 70))} != catalog=${JSON.stringify(want.slice(0, 70))}`);
            const txt = el.textContent.trim();
            if (KEY.test(txt)) out.rawKeys.push(`${key} (raw key visible)`);
            else if (/\{[a-zA-Z]\w*\}/.test(txt)) out.rawKeys.push(`${key} (un-interpolated placeholder)`);
            else if (/\bundefined\b|\[object Object\]/.test(txt)) out.rawKeys.push(`${key} (undefined/object)`);
            if (!htmlKey && txt.length === 0) out.empties.push(key);
          }
          return out;
        }, { loc, KEY_SRC: KEY_RE.source });

        if (r.htmlLang !== loc) failures.push(`${at}: <html lang>="${r.htmlLang}", expected "${loc}"`);
        for (const m of r.mismatches) failures.push(`${at}: ${m}`);
        for (const k of r.rawKeys) failures.push(`${at}: ${k}`);
        for (const k of r.empties) failures.push(`${at}: "${k}" rendered EMPTY`);
        if (SCRIPT[loc]) {
          const body = await page.evaluate(() => document.body.innerText);
          if (!SCRIPT[loc].test(body)) failures.push(`${at}: no ${loc} script present in the rendered page (translation/glyph path broken)`);
        }
      } finally { await ctx.close(); }
    }
  }
  assert.equal(failures.length, 0, failures.length ? `render inconsistencies (${failures.length}):\n  - ${failures.join("\n  - ")}\n` : "");
});

289	  ).window;
290	  const nav = document.querySelector(".nav");
291	  // jsdom returns 0 for both; override the instance getters to simulate real layout.
292	  const geom = (scrollWidth, clientWidth) => {
293	    Object.defineProperty(nav, "scrollWidth", { value: scrollWidth, configurable: true });
294	    Object.defineProperty(nav, "clientWidth", { value: clientWidth, configurable: true });
295	  };
296	
297	  geom(1200, 1000); // content wider than the box -> the 6 links don't fit
298	  fitNav(document);
299	  assert.ok(nav.classList.contains("nav-crowded"), "overflow must collapse the nav (add .nav-crowded)");
300	
301	  geom(1000, 1000); // now it fits
302	  fitNav(document);
303	  assert.ok(!nav.classList.contains("nav-crowded"), "fitting again must reveal the links (drop .nav-crowded)");
304	
305	  fitNav(document); // re-running on a fitting nav is a no-op (no flip-flop)
306	  assert.ok(!nav.classList.contains("nav-crowded"), "fitNav must be idempotent");

// Visual evidence for the two newly-localized home-page surfaces (the access form #join + the footer),
// captured per locale. Complements the automated render guard with eyeball-able stills.
import { chromium } from "playwright";
const URL = process.env.URL || "http://localhost:8132";
const browser = await chromium.launch();
const errs = {};
for (const loc of ["en", "kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"]) {
  const ctx = await browser.newContext({ viewport: { width: 1000, height: 900 } });
  const page = await ctx.newPage();
  const e = []; page.on("console", (m) => m.type() === "error" && e.push(m.text())); page.on("pageerror", (x) => e.push(String(x)));
  await page.goto(`${URL}/?lang=${loc}`, { waitUntil: "networkidle" });
  await page.waitForFunction((l) => document.querySelector("[data-langsw]")?.value === l, loc, { timeout: 6000 }).catch(() => {});
  await page.evaluate(() => (document.fonts ? document.fonts.ready.then(() => true) : true));
  await page.locator("#join").scrollIntoViewIfNeeded().catch(() => {});
  await page.waitForTimeout(500);
  await page.locator("#af").screenshot({ path: `jf-form-${loc}.png` }).catch(async () => { await page.screenshot({ path: `jf-form-${loc}.png` }); });
  await page.locator(".foot, footer").first().scrollIntoViewIfNeeded().catch(() => {});
  await page.waitForTimeout(400);
  await page.locator("footer").screenshot({ path: `jf-footer-${loc}.png` }).catch(() => {});
  errs[loc] = e.filter((x) => !/favicon/i.test(x));
  await ctx.close();
}
await browser.close();
console.log("console errors by locale:", JSON.stringify(errs));
console.log("wrote jf-form-{en,kn,hi,es,da,id}.png + jf-footer-*.png");

### 410. assistant (2026-06-22T16:16:20.175Z)

// One-off helper (NOT shipped): author + seed the FULL access-form localization.
//  (1) jsdom-extract every site.join.* + site.footer.* value byte-for-byte from the wired index.html;
//  (2) add the JS-only site.join.sending key;
//  (3) en = authoritative; non-en: seed any NOT-yet-translated key with en (preserve existing translations);
//  (4) emit the payload = the keys still needing translation (en==locale) + per-locale register refs.
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { JSDOM } from "jsdom";

const ROOT = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..");
const load = (l) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, "locales", l, "common.json"), "utf8"));
const html = readFileSync(join(ROOT, "site", "public", "index.html"), "utf8");
const { document } = new JSDOM(html).window;

// 1) extract every site.join.* / site.footer.* default from the page (byte-for-byte)
const enVals = { "site.join.sending": "Sending…" }; // JS-only key (not a data-i18n element)
for (const el of document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n^="site.join."], [data-i18n^="site.footer."], [data-i18n-html^="site.join."], [data-i18n-html^="site.footer."]')) {
  if (el.getAttribute("data-i18n-attr")) continue;
  const hk = el.getAttribute("data-i18n-html");
  const key = hk || el.getAttribute("data-i18n");
  enVals[key] = (hk ? el.innerHTML : el.textContent).trim();
}
const setDeep = (root, dotted, v) => { const p = dotted.split("."); let o = root; for (let i = 0; i < p.length - 1; i++) o = o[p[i]] = o[p[i]] || {}; o[p[p.length - 1]] = v; };
const getDeep = (root, dotted) => dotted.split(".").reduce((a, c) => (a == null ? a : a[c]), root);

// 2) en authoritative; 3) non-en seed untranslated keys (preserve existing translations)
for (const loc of ["en", "kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"]) {
  const cat = load(loc);
  for (const [key, v] of Object.entries(enVals)) {
    const cur = getDeep(cat, key);
    if (loc === "en" || cur == null) setDeep(cat, key, v); // en: overwrite; others: only fill missing
  }
  writeFileSync(join(ROOT, "locales", loc, "common.json"), JSON.stringify(cat, null, 2) + "\n");
}

// 4) payload: keys still untranslated in kn (kn == en) — i.e. the brand-new form keys + sending
const en = load("en"), kn = load("kn");
const target = {};
for (const key of Object.keys(enVals)) if (getDeep(kn, key) === getDeep(en, key)) target[key] = enVals[key];
const refOf = (c) => ({ app_title: c.app.title, grid_rally: c.grid.rally, lang_label: c.lang.label,
  site_nav: c.site.nav, site_hero_eyebrow: c.site.hero.eyebrow,
  join_gpuLabel: c.site.join.gpuLabel, join_gpuYes: c.site.join.gpuYes }); // show how the form's existing keys read
const payload = { enTargets: target, ref: { kn: refOf(load("kn")), hi: refOf(load("hi")), es: refOf(load("es")), da: refOf(load("da")), id: refOf(load("id")) } };
writeFileSync(join(ROOT, "site", "scripts", "_payload2.json"), JSON.stringify(payload));
console.error(`extracted ${Object.keys(enVals).length} site.join/footer keys; ${Object.keys(target).length} still need translation`);
process.stdout.write(Object.keys(target).join("\n") + "\n");

// One-off helper (NOT shipped): merge the rest-of-form translations (_jf2.json) into kn/hi/es/da/id.
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
const ROOT = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..");
const load = (l) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, "locales", l, "common.json"), "utf8"));
const EXPECTED = Object.keys(JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, "site", "scripts", "_payload2.json"), "utf8")).enTargets);
const T = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, "site", "scripts", "_jf2.json"), "utf8"));
const setDeep = (root, dotted, v) => { const p = dotted.split("."); let o = root; for (let i = 0; i < p.length - 1; i++) o = o[p[i]] = o[p[i]] || {}; o[p[p.length - 1]] = v; };
let bad = 0;
for (const loc of ["kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"]) {
  const f = T[loc];
  if (!f) { console.error(`MISSING ${loc}`); bad++; continue; }
  const missing = EXPECTED.filter((k) => !(k in f));
  if (missing.length) { console.error(`${loc} MISSING:`, missing.join(", ")); bad++; }
  const cat = load(loc);
  for (const k of EXPECTED) if (k in f) setDeep(cat, k, f[k]);
  writeFileSync(join(ROOT, "locales", loc, "common.json"), JSON.stringify(cat, null, 2) + "\n");
  console.log(`${loc}: applied ${EXPECTED.filter((k) => k in f).length}/${EXPECTED.length}`);
}
if (bad) process.exit(1);
console.log("OK");

## What this turn delivered
- **Synced to remote head** and caught that a lot landed from sibling sessions (#88–#104, incl. the hero-mockup chip I'd spawned and the `fitNav` layout guard you mentioned).
- **Completed the access form + footer** — eyeballing the rendered Kannada page revealed the form was only ⅓ wired (A6 had done just the GPU/storage questions); the whole rest was hardcoded English. Wired + translated **26 form keys + 6 footer keys** ×5 langs (incl. the JS "Sending…" via the shim bridge), AI-draft → 3-lens verify → revise.
- **Found a real inconsistency by eyeballing** that the render guard couldn't see: Hindi mixed `Bengaluru`/`बेंगलुरु`. Fixed + pinned with a proper-noun test.
- **Render-tested every locale page + locked it in:** new `test:render` (headless Chromium over all 18 page×locale combos: live-DOM == catalog, right `<html lang>`, no console errors, Indic script) wired into CI; extended the layout guard (footer/form overflow); added a deterministic no-bare-strings guard (catches the un-wired-English class that hid the form). Verified: site 92 + layout 3 + render 1, web 226, typecheck/coverage 93.8%/build — all green.
- **Concurrency-safe:** rebased onto `#104` (compute-picker) that landed mid-work; confirmed the merge kept both its keys and mine (key-parity + its tests green).

The two human gates you cleared + this work mean the chapter is effectively **closed** (archived in #93; only the deferred cross-repo **B7** remains).

## Prioritized list — remaining localization & friendliness AIs

**P1 — small, high-value (recommend next)**
1. **Localize the Privacy Policy page** (`privacy.html`, added in #96). It's the *only* English surface a non-English user still hits — it's linked from the footer (every page) *and* the form's now-translated "See our Privacy Policy" note, so an es/kn user lands on an English legal page. Wire shim+switcher + translate ×5 (~193 lines; legal tone). *Decision flag: some teams keep one authoritative legal language — your call whether to translate or just add the switcher + an "authoritative English" note.*
2. **Native re-review of the post-approval strings** — the #105 form/footer (and Privacy, once done) were added *after* your native sign-off, so they're AI-drafted. A quick native pass on just the delta keeps the quality bar.

**P2 — medium**
3. **Per-locale `<title>` / meta description / `og:image`** — all pages currently ship English titles + social-share metadata, so non-English shares/SEO show English. Localize the head metadata (+ a localized OG image per language).
4. **A short localized "how to record" explainer** (video/animated) — the capture page has text + the A7 diagram, but a low-literacy, non-tech coach benefits from a wordless/voiced walkthrough in their language. Directly serves the "friendly" goal.
5. **Cross-locale accessibility audit** — contrast, screen-reader labels, and `lang` attributes on mixed-script runs (e.g. Kannada prose with Latin "KhelSutra"/"VLC") across all 6 locales.

**P3 — large / deferred / owner-gated**
6. **B7 — backend (engine) customer-facing l10n** (report `customer_*` text, rejection reasons). Cross-repo Python, owner-gated (D-BACKEND-DEPTH). The last tracked deferral.
7. **Kannada/Hindi query *input* in the SPA** (typing/conversational queries in-language) — today it's English-grammar parsing + tap-only filters (A9). This is I18N_PLAN P4, a real NLP feature; render/i18n tests become a merge gate when it lands.
8. **es/da/id academy flyers** — print flyers exist only for en/kn/hi. Add the other three only if those markets matter (the beachhead is Bengaluru).
9. **Localized request-access confirmation email** — the VM host currently uses `noopNotify` (no email yet); when email delivery lands, send it in the requester's language.

Happy to take **P1.1 (Privacy page)** next — it's the cleanest remaining win and closes the last non-English surface. Want me to start there, or pick differently?

72	<body>
73	
74	<header>
75	  <div class="nav">
76	    <a class="brand" href="/">Khel<span class="play" aria-hidden="true"></span>Sutra</a>
77	    <a class="back" href="/">← Back to KhelSutra</a>
78	  </div>
79	</header>
80	
81	<section class="hero">
82	  <div class="wrap">
83	    <h1>Privacy Policy</h1>
84	    <p class="sub">How KhelSutra handles your information and your match videos — and the choices you have.</p>
85	    <span class="updated">Last updated: 22 June 2026</span>
86	  </div>
87	</section>
88	
89	<main>
90	  <div class="wrap">
91	    <p class="lead">KhelSutra ("we", "us") turns a full badminton match into a clean highlight reel. We're an invite-only alpha run from Bengaluru, India. This page explains, in plain language, what information we collect, how we use it, who else touches it, and how you stay in control. Questions? Email <a href="mailto:hello@khelsutra.guru">hello@khelsutra.guru</a>.</p>
92	
93	    <div class="toc">
94	      <h2>On this page</h2>
95	      <ol>
96	        <li><a href="#collect">What we collect</a></li>
97	        <li><a href="#use">How we use it</a></li>
98	        <li><a href="#video">Your videos &amp; how they're processed</a></li>
99	        <li><a href="#sharing">Who else touches your data</a></li>
100	        <li><a href="#cookies">Cookies &amp; analytics</a></li>
101	        <li><a href="#retention">How long we keep it</a></li>
102	        <li><a href="#rights">Your choices &amp; rights</a></li>
103	        <li><a href="#minors">Minors</a></li>
104	        <li><a href="#security">Security</a></li>
105	        <li><a href="#changes">Changes &amp; contact</a></li>
106	      </ol>
107	    </div>
108	
109	    <h2 id="collect">1. What we collect</h2>
110	    <p>We only collect what we need to run the alpha and produce your reels:</p>
111	    <ul>
112	      <li><b>Request-access details</b> — when you fill in the access form: your name and email, and optionally your city, academy/club name, how many courts you record, whether you have a powerful (GPU) computer, where your videos live, and any message you send us.</li>
113	      <li><b>Match videos you choose to give us</b> — the footage you upload or share so we can index the rallies and build your reel.</li>
114	      <li><b>Basic technical data</b> — like your approximate country and browser type, handled by our hosting and edge-security provider (Cloudflare) to keep the site working and safe.</li>
115	    </ul>
116	    <p>We do <b>not</b> ask for, or want, sensitive personal data, and we don't run intrusive cross-site advertising trackers.</p>
117	
118	    <h2 id="use">2. How we use it</h2>
119	    <ul>
120	      <li>To <b>contact you about the alpha</b> — but only if you ticked the consent box on the form.</li>
121	      <li>To <b>provide the service</b> — to index your match, strip the dead time, and generate the reel you download.</li>
122	      <li>To <b>understand demand and improve the product experience</b> in aggregate (e.g. how many academies vs. individuals are interested).</li>
123	    </ul>
124	    <p>We do <b>not</b> sell, rent, or trade your personal data. We do <b>not</b> use your footage to train or improve our models <b>unless you give us clear, explicit, opt-in permission</b> — and even then, never footage involving minors.</p>
125	
126	    <h2 id="video">3. Your videos &amp; how they're processed</h2>
127	    <p>Your match videos are large files that need real computing to analyse, so here's exactly what happens to them:</p>
128	    <ul>
129	      <li>We process your video on our own infrastructure to find the rallies and build your reel.</li>
130	      <li>As part of the analysis we may use <b>trusted third-party AI and cloud services</b> (for example, Google's Gemini API) to detect rallies. When we do, we send only the footage needed for that step.</li>
131	      <li>We <b>never publish</b> your footage, and we never make it public.</li>
132	      <li>We never use your footage to improve the tool without your explicit opt-in (see above).</li>
133	    </ul>
134	    <div class="note">
135	      <b>If you run your own model:</b>
136	      <p>The "own your model" path lets advanced users process footage on their own hardware. In that case your video stays on your machine and is not sent to us or to third-party AI services.</p>
137	    </div>
138	
139	    <h2 id="sharing">4. Who else touches your data</h2>
140	    <p>We keep the list of processors small and name them honestly:</p>
141	    <ul>
142	      <li><b>Cloudflare</b> — hosts the website, secures it at the edge, and provides privacy-friendly analytics.</li>
143	      <li><b>Google (Gemini API)</b> — used, when applicable, to analyse footage and detect rallies.</li>
144	      <li><b>Email delivery</b> — to send the messages you asked us to send about the alpha.</li>
145	    </ul>
146	    <p>These providers act on our behalf to deliver the service. We don't give anyone your data to use for their own marketing.</p>
147	
148	    <h2 id="cookies">5. Cookies &amp; analytics</h2>
149	    <p>We keep this light:</p>
150	    <ul>
151	      <li>We store a single <b>language preference</b> in your browser so the site remembers the language you picked. That's a functional preference, not a tracking cookie.</li>
152	      <li>We use <b>privacy-first analytics</b> (Cloudflare Web Analytics) to count visits and see which pages are useful. It does not use tracking cookies and does not build a profile of you across other sites.</li>
153	    </ul>
154	
155	    <h2 id="retention">6. How long we keep it</h2>
156	    <p>We keep your information and footage only as long as we need it to run the alpha and provide the service, and then delete or anonymise it. You can ask us to delete your uploaded videos and your account details at any time — see your rights below.</p>
157	
158	    <h2 id="rights">7. Your choices &amp; rights</h2>
159	    <p>You're in control. At any time you can ask us to:</p>
160	    <ul>
161	      <li>See what personal data we hold about you;</li>
162	      <li>Correct anything that's wrong;</li>
163	      <li>Delete your data and uploaded footage;</li>
164	      <li>Withdraw your consent to be emailed (every email we send also has an easy opt-out).</li>
165	    </ul>
166	    <p>To exercise any of these, just email <a href="mailto:hello@khelsutra.guru">hello@khelsutra.guru</a> and we'll take care of it.</p>
167	
168	    <h2 id="minors">8. Minors</h2>
169	    <p>We take footage involving minors seriously. Footage involving minors is <b>never</b> used to improve the tool and is <b>never</b> published — full stop. If you believe a minor's personal data has been shared with us inappropriately, contact us and we'll remove it.</p>
170	
171	    <h2 id="security">9. Security</h2>
172	    <p>The site is served only over <b>HTTPS</b> with an auto-renewing TLS certificate, so your connection is encrypted. The alpha app itself is gated behind an authenticated, invite-only sign-in, and access to the processing system is restricted. No system is perfectly secure, but we take reasonable, layered measures to protect your data.</p>
173	
174	    <h2 id="changes">10. Changes &amp; contact</h2>
175	    <p>This policy reflects our current alpha practices and may change as the product evolves; we'll update the "last updated" date above when it does. For any privacy question or request, email <a href="mailto:hello@khelsutra.guru">hello@khelsutra.guru</a>.</p>
176	  </div>
177	</main>
178	
179	<footer>
180	  <div class="wrap foot">
181	    <div>
182	      <a class="brand" href="/" style="font-size:1.05rem">Khel<span class="play" aria-hidden="true"></span>Sutra</a>
183	      <div class="stamp">Automatic badminton highlights · Bengaluru, India</div>
184	    </div>
185	    <div style="text-align:right">
186	      <div><a href="/">Home</a> · <a href="mailto:hello@khelsutra.guru">hello@khelsutra.guru</a> · <a href="https://khelsutra.guru">khelsutra.guru</a></div>
187	      <div class="stamp">🔒 Served securely over HTTPS · © 2026 KhelSutra · Invite-only alpha</div>
188	    </div>
189	  </div>
190	</footer>
191	
192	</body>
193	</html>

// One-off helper (NOT shipped): author + seed site.privacy.* from the wired privacy.html (byte-for-byte),
// then emit the translation payload (all site.privacy.* keys) + per-locale register refs.
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { JSDOM } from "jsdom";

const ROOT = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..");
const load = (l) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, "locales", l, "common.json"), "utf8"));
const { document } = new JSDOM(readFileSync(join(ROOT, "site", "public", "privacy.html"), "utf8")).window;

const en = {};
for (const el of document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n^="site.privacy."], [data-i18n-html^="site.privacy."]')) {
  if (el.getAttribute("data-i18n-attr")) continue;
  const hk = el.getAttribute("data-i18n-html");
  const key = hk || el.getAttribute("data-i18n");
  en[key] = (hk ? el.innerHTML : el.textContent).trim();
}
const setDeep = (root, dotted, v) => { const p = dotted.split("."); let o = root; for (let i = 0; i < p.length - 1; i++) o = o[p[i]] = o[p[i]] || {}; o[p[p.length - 1]] = v; };
const getDeep = (root, dotted) => dotted.split(".").reduce((a, c) => (a == null ? a : a[c]), root);

for (const loc of ["en", "kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"]) {
  const cat = load(loc);
  for (const [key, v] of Object.entries(en)) if (loc === "en" || getDeep(cat, key) == null) setDeep(cat, key, v);
  writeFileSync(join(ROOT, "locales", loc, "common.json"), JSON.stringify(cat, null, 2) + "\n");
}

const refOf = (c) => ({ lang_label: c.lang.label, site_nav_ownModel: c.site.nav.ownModel, site_footer_tagline: c.site.footer.tagline,
  join_consent: c.site.join.consent, join_note: c.site.join.note, ownModel_train_minorsBody: c.site.ownModel.train.moat.minorsBody });
const payload = { enTargets: en, ref: { kn: refOf(load("kn")), hi: refOf(load("hi")), es: refOf(load("es")), da: refOf(load("da")), id: refOf(load("id")) } };
writeFileSync(join(ROOT, "site", "scripts", "_privpayload.json"), JSON.stringify(payload));
console.error(`extracted ${Object.keys(en).length} site.privacy.* keys; payload bytes ${JSON.stringify(payload).length}`);
process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(en, null, 1).slice(0, 600) + "\n…\n");

### 524. assistant (2026-06-22T17:08:39.637Z)

// One-off helper (NOT shipped): merge the privacy translations (_priv.json) into kn/hi/es/da/id.
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
const ROOT = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..");
const load = (l) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, "locales", l, "common.json"), "utf8"));
const EXPECTED = Object.keys(JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, "site", "scripts", "_privpayload.json"), "utf8")).enTargets);
const T = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, "site", "scripts", "_priv.json"), "utf8"));
const setDeep = (root, dotted, v) => { const p = dotted.split("."); let o = root; for (let i = 0; i < p.length - 1; i++) o = o[p[i]] = o[p[i]] || {}; o[p[p.length - 1]] = v; };
let bad = 0;
for (const loc of ["kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"]) {
  const f = T[loc];
  if (!f) { console.error(`MISSING ${loc}`); bad++; continue; }
  const missing = EXPECTED.filter((k) => !(k in f));
  if (missing.length) { console.error(`${loc} MISSING:`, missing.join(", ")); bad++; }
  const cat = load(loc);
  for (const k of EXPECTED) if (k in f) setDeep(cat, k, f[k]);
  writeFileSync(join(ROOT, "locales", loc, "common.json"), JSON.stringify(cat, null, 2) + "\n");
  console.log(`${loc}: applied ${EXPECTED.filter((k) => k in f).length}/${EXPECTED.length}`);
}
if (bad) process.exit(1);
console.log("OK");

308	test("A3: EVERY data-i18n[-html] HTML default matches the en catalog byte-for-byte (no drift, incl. rich <b> keys)", () => {
309	  // The rich (data-i18n-html) keys are the most drift-prone: the inline <b> markup must match the
310	  // catalog exactly, or an en visitor's no-JS default and the shim's en render silently diverge.
311	  // Parse the REAL pages in jsdom and compare each tagged element's default to enCatalog.
312	  const dig = (o, k) => k.split(".").reduce((a, c) => (a == null ? a : a[c]), o);
313	  let checked = 0;
314	  for (const f of ["index.html", "capture.html", "own-model.html"]) {
315	    const { document } = new JSDOM(read(f)).window;
316	    for (const el of document.querySelectorAll("[data-i18n], [data-i18n-html]")) {
317	      if (el.getAttribute("data-i18n-attr")) continue; // attribute defaults aren't the visible text
318	      const htmlKey = el.getAttribute("data-i18n-html");
319	      const key = htmlKey || el.getAttribute("data-i18n");
320	      const enVal = dig(enCatalog, key);
321	      assert.notEqual(enVal, undefined, `${f}: data-i18n key "${key}" missing from en catalog`);
322	      const got = (htmlKey ? el.innerHTML : el.textContent).trim();
323	      assert.equal(got, enVal, `${f}: "${key}" HTML default drifted from the en catalog`);
324	      checked++;
325	    }
326	  }
327	  assert.ok(checked >= 60, `expected to check the full localized surface, only saw ${checked}`);
328	});

### 587. assistant (2026-06-22T17:28:24.487Z)

// Accessibility regression guard (separate `npm run test:a11y` — needs a real browser, like the layout
// + render guards). Runs axe-core (WCAG 2.1 A/AA) over every localized page in representative locales
// (en = Latin, kn = Kannada, hi = Devanagari) and FAILS on any serious/critical violation. jsdom has no
// layout/contrast engine, so this is the only automated a11y net. It complements the manual review.
import { test, before, after } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { createServer } from "node:http";
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { join, extname } from "node:path";
import { chromium } from "playwright";

const PUBLIC = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "public");
const AXE = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "node_modules", "axe-core", "axe.min.js");
const TYPES = {
  ".html": "text/html; charset=utf-8", ".js": "text/javascript", ".mjs": "text/javascript",
  ".json": "application/json; charset=utf-8", ".css": "text/css", ".png": "image/png", ".jpg": "image/jpeg",
  ".svg": "image/svg+xml", ".webp": "image/webp", ".webm": "video/webm", ".mp4": "video/mp4",
  ".woff2": "font/woff2", ".woff": "font/woff", ".ico": "image/x-icon",
};
let server, browser, baseURL, axeSrc;

before(async () => {
  axeSrc = await readFile(AXE, "utf8");
  server = createServer(async (req, res) => {
    try {
      let p = decodeURIComponent(req.url.split("?")[0]);
      if (p === "/") p = "/index.html";
      let data, ext = extname(p);
      try { data = await readFile(join(PUBLIC, p)); }
      catch { try { data = await readFile(join(PUBLIC, p + ".html")); ext = ".html"; } catch { res.writeHead(404); return res.end("not found"); } }
      res.writeHead(200, { "content-type": TYPES[ext] || "application/octet-stream", "cache-control": "no-store" });
      res.end(data);
    } catch (e) { res.writeHead(500); res.end(String(e)); }
  });
  await new Promise((r) => server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", r));
  baseURL = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`;
  browser = await chromium.launch();
});
after(async () => { await browser?.close(); await new Promise((r) => server?.close(r)); });

const PAGES = ["/index.html", "/capture.html", "/own-model.html", "/privacy.html"];
const LOCALES = ["en", "kn", "hi"]; // Latin + Kannada + Devanagari (es/da/id share en's Latin markup)

test("axe-core WCAG 2.1 A/AA — no serious/critical violations on any localized page × locale", async () => {
  const failures = [];
  for (const path of PAGES) {
    for (const loc of LOCALES) {
      const ctx = await browser.newContext();
      const page = await ctx.newPage();
      try {
        await page.goto(`${baseURL}${path}?lang=${loc}`, { waitUntil: "load" });
        await page.waitForFunction((l) => document.querySelector("[data-langsw]")?.value === l, loc, { timeout: 6000 }).catch(() => {});
        await page.evaluate(() => (document.fonts ? document.fonts.ready.then(() => true) : true));
        await page.addScriptTag({ content: axeSrc });
        const results = await page.evaluate(async () => {
          // WCAG 2.1 A + AA rule sets; report serious/critical only (the actionable bar).
          const r = await window.axe.run(document, { runOnly: { type: "tag", values: ["wcag2a", "wcag2aa", "wcag21a", "wcag21aa"] } });
          return r.violations.map((v) => ({ id: v.id, impact: v.impact, help: v.help, n: v.nodes.length, sample: (v.nodes[0]?.target || []).join(" ") }));
        });
        for (const v of results.filter((v) => v.impact === "serious" || v.impact === "critical")) {
          failures.push(`${path} [${loc}]: ${v.impact} — ${v.id} (${v.help}) ×${v.n} e.g. ${v.sample}`);
        }
      } finally { await ctx.close(); }
    }
  }
  assert.equal(failures.length, 0, failures.length ? `axe serious/critical violations:\n  - ${failures.join("\n  - ")}\n` : "");
});

// One-off helper (NOT shipped): merge the per-page <title> translations (_meta.json) into kn/hi/es/da/id.
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
const ROOT = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..");
const load = (l) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, "locales", l, "common.json"), "utf8"));
const EXPECTED = Object.keys(JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, "site", "scripts", "_metapayload.json"), "utf8")).enTargets);
const T = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, "site", "scripts", "_meta.json"), "utf8"));
const setDeep = (root, dotted, v) => { const p = dotted.split("."); let o = root; for (let i = 0; i < p.length - 1; i++) o = o[p[i]] = o[p[i]] || {}; o[p[p.length - 1]] = v; };
let bad = 0;
for (const loc of ["kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"]) {
  const f = T[loc] && T[loc].final;
  if (!f) { console.error(`MISSING ${loc}`); bad++; continue; }
  const missing = EXPECTED.filter((k) => !(k in f));
  if (missing.length) { console.error(`${loc} MISSING:`, missing.join(", ")); bad++; }
  const cat = load(loc);
  for (const k of EXPECTED) if (k in f) setDeep(cat, k, f[k]);
  writeFileSync(join(ROOT, "locales", loc, "common.json"), JSON.stringify(cat, null, 2) + "\n");
  console.log(`${loc}: ${EXPECTED.filter((k) => k in f).length}/${EXPECTED.length} | index="${f["site.meta.index.title"]}"`);
}

=== flyer.html (en) ===
<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8" />
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" />
<title>KhelSutra — try it free, tell us what you think (academy flyer)</title>
<meta name="description" content="Pin-up flyer for badminton academies: record your session, KhelSutra kills the dead time automatically, saves you hours, and hands you share-ready highlight clips. Try the alpha and give feedback." />
<link rel="icon" href="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg' viewBox='0 0 32 32'%3E%3Crect width='32' height='32' rx='8' fill='%230C261F'/%3E%3Cpath d='M13 10 L23 16 L13 22 Z' fill='%2319B36B'/%3E%3C/svg%3E" />
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" />
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin />
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Plus+Jakarta+Sans:wght@400;500;600;700;800&display=swap" rel="stylesheet" />
<style>
  :root{
    --ink:#0C261F; --ink2:#123A2E; --green:#19B36B; --green-d:#137a4c;
    --lime:#C7F2B0; --paper:#F6F8F4; --tx:#14271F; --mut:#566B61; --line:#E1E7DF;
    --font:'Plus Jakarta Sans',system-ui,-apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif;
  }
  *{box-sizing:border-box}
  body{margin:0;font-family:var(--font);color:var(--tx);background:#E9EDE6;line-height:1.45;-webkit-font-smoothing:antialiased}

  /* A4 portrait sheet */
  .sheet{width:210mm;min-height:297mm;margin:14px auto;background:#fff;border-radius:14px;overflow:hidden;
    box-shadow:0 18px 50px -24px rgba(0,0,0,.4);display:flex;flex-direction:column}

  /* header / hero */
  .head{background:linear-gradient(160deg,var(--ink) 0%,var(--ink2) 100%);color:#EAF3EE;padding:16px 34px 17px}
  .topline{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:1rem}
  .brand{display:flex;align-items:center;font-weight:800;font-size:1.5rem;letter-spacing:-.02em}
  .brand .play{display:inline-block;width:0;height:0;border-style:solid;border-width:8px 0 8px 13px;
    border-color:transparent transparent transparent var(--green);margin:0 .32em;transform:translateY(1px)}
  .eyebrow{font-size:.74rem;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.05em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--lime);
    background:rgba(199,242,176,.12);border:1px solid rgba(199,242,176,.25);padding:.34rem .7rem;border-radius:999px;white-space:nowrap}
  .head h1{font-size:2.35rem;font-weight:800;line-height:1.06;margin:16px 0 0;max-width:18ch;letter-spacing:-.02em}
  .head h1 .hl{color:var(--lime)}
  .head .lead{color:#BcD8Cb;font-size:1rem;margin:.7rem 0 0;max-width:60ch}

  .body{padding:14px 34px 4px;flex:1;display:flex;flex-direction:column}

  /* spotlight: kill the dead time */
  .spot{display:flex;gap:1rem;align-items:center;background:#EAF6EF;border:1px solid #BfE6D2;border-radius:13px;
    padding:13px 18px;margin-bottom:15px}
  .spot .big{flex:none;text-align:center;padding-right:16px;border-right:1px solid #BfE6D2}
  .spot .big b{display:block;font-size:2rem;font-weight:800;color:var(--green-d);line-height:1}
  .spot .big em{font-style:normal;font-size:.72rem;color:var(--mut);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.04em}
  .spot p{margin:0;font-size:.98rem;color:var(--tx)}
  .spot p b{color:var(--green-d)}
  .track{position:relative;height:14px;border-radius:7px;background:#dDE6dE;margin:.5rem 0 .2rem;overflow:hidden}
  .track span{position:absolute;top:0;bottom:0;background:var(--green);border-radius:7px}
  .tracklbl{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:.74rem;color:var(--mut)}

  /* outcome chips */
  .chips{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:12px;margin-bottom:16px}
  .chip{display:flex;gap:.6rem;align-items:center;background:#fff;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:11px;padding:11px 13px}
  .chip .ic{width:36px;height:36px;border-radius:10px;flex:none;background:rgba(25,179,107,.12);color:var(--green-d);
    display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:1.25rem}
  .chip b{display:block;font-size:.96rem;color:var(--tx)}
  .chip span{font-size:.8rem;color:var(--mut)}

  /* compact record guide */
  .guide{background:var(--paper);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:13px;padding:14px 18px;margin-bottom:16px}
  .guide .gh{display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:.55rem;margin-bottom:.55rem;flex-wrap:wrap}
  .guide .gh .k{font-size:.74rem;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--green-d)}
  .guide .gh h2{font-size:1.12rem;font-weight:700;margin:0}
  .golden{display:flex;gap:.55rem;align-items:flex-start;margin-bottom:.65rem}
  .golden .star{color:var(--green-d);font-size:1.2rem;line-height:1.05}
  .golden p{margin:0;font-size:.93rem;color:var(--tx)}
  .ess{list-style:none;margin:0;padding:0;display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:.35rem 1.4rem}
  .ess li{display:flex;gap:.45rem;font-size:.89rem;color:#34453d}
  .ess li i{color:var(--green-d);font-style:normal;font-weight:700;margin-top:.05rem}
  .ess li b{color:var(--tx)}
  .guide .more{margin:.7rem 0 0;font-size:.83rem;color:var(--mut)}
  .guide .more a{color:var(--green-d);text-decoration:none;font-weight:600}

  /* CTA / feedback band */
  .cta{margin-top:auto;background:linear-gradient(160deg,var(--ink) 0%,var(--ink2) 100%);color:#EAF3EE;
    border-radius:15px;padding:18px 22px;display:flex;gap:22px;align-items:center}
  .cta-tx{flex:1}
  .cta-tx .pill{display:inline-block;background:rgba(199,242,176,.14);color:var(--lime);font-size:.72rem;font-weight:700;
    letter-spacing:.04em;text-transform:uppercase;padding:.3rem .72rem;border-radius:999px;margin-bottom:.55rem}
  .cta-tx h2{font-size:1.42rem;font-weight:800;margin:0;color:#fff;line-height:1.14}
