### 689. assistant (2026-06-22T17:52:44.856Z)

// One-off helper (NOT shipped): build flyer-es/da/id.html from flyer.html + the translations (_flyer.json).
// The flyer is a STANDALONE hardcoded-language page (not the data-i18n shim) — like flyer-kn/flyer-hi — so we
// bake the translations into a copy: swap <html lang>, the studio screenshot (src + localized alt), and every
// translatable English string. Replacement is exact-substring on the raw HTML, with bare "&" encoded to "&amp;"
// so it matches the source, longest-first so no short string clobbers a longer one.
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";

const ROOT = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..");
const SITE = join(ROOT, "site");
const en = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(SITE, "scripts", "_flyer_en.json"), "utf8"));
const T = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(SITE, "scripts", "_flyer.json"), "utf8"));
const flyer = readFileSync(join(SITE, "public", "flyer.html"), "utf8");
const cat = (l) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, "locales", l, "common.json"), "utf8"));

const encAmp = (s) => String(s).replace(/&(?!amp;|lt;|gt;|quot;|#)/g, "&amp;"); // bare & -> &amp; (don't double-encode)
const EN_ALT = "KhelSutra Studio — pick rallies, build a reel";

let report = [];
for (const loc of ["es", "da", "id"]) {
  const tr = T[loc];
  if (!tr) { report.push(`${loc}: MISSING translations`); continue; }
  const missing = Object.keys(en).filter((k) => !(k in tr));
  if (missing.length) report.push(`${loc}: MISSING keys ${missing.join(", ")}`);

  let html = flyer;
  // 1) <html lang>
  html = html.replace(/<html lang="en">/, `<html lang="${loc}">`);
  // 2) studio screenshot src + localized alt (reuse the SPA's own translated title)
  html = html.split("/img/studio-en.png").join(`/img/studio-${loc}.png`);
  const alt = `KhelSutra Studio — ${cat(loc).app.title}`;
  html = html.split(EN_ALT).join(alt);
  // 3) every translatable string, longest en first (encode bare & to match the HTML source)
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(en).sort((a, b) => b[1].length - a[1].length)) {
    if (!(k in tr)) continue;
    const from = encAmp(v), to = encAmp(tr[k]);
    if (!html.includes(from)) { report.push(`${loc}: en string for "${k}" not found verbatim in flyer.html (skipped)`); continue; }
    html = html.split(from).join(to);
  }
  writeFileSync(join(SITE, "public", `flyer-${loc}.html`), html);
  // 4) sanity: a few English sentinels must be gone
  const leftovers = ["The golden rule", "Save hours", "Record it right", "dead time gone"].filter((s) => html.includes(s));
  report.push(`${loc}: wrote flyer-${loc}.html (${html.length}b)${leftovers.length ? " ⚠ leftover EN: " + leftovers.join(", ") : " — no EN sentinel left"}`);
}
console.log(report.join("\n"));

=== docs/MULTILINGUAL_EXPANSION.md head ===
# Multilingual Expansion — 7 new locales (th · id · da · es · zh-Hans · ja · ko)

> **Status:** `IN PROGRESS` — **Phase A (es/da/id) shipped ([#67](https://github.com/avidullu/khelsutra/pull/67)); Phases B (Thai) / C (CJK) owner-gated** · **Owner:** Avi Dullu · **Created:** 2026-06-21 · **Last updated:** 2026-06-21
> **Lifecycle:** `DRAFT → IN PROGRESS → DONE → archived` (move to `docs/archives/` when DONE)
> **Tracking anchors:** §7 progress tracker is the source of truth; index in `docs/README.md`; pointer in memory `current-state`; thread in `NEXT_STEPS.md` once work starts.
> **Relation to existing docs:** **PEER-OF / extends** [`FRIENDLY_AND_MULTILINGUAL.md`](archives/FRIENDLY_AND_MULTILINGUAL.md) (the `en`/`kn`/`hi` foundation) and the engine design `I18N_PLAN.md`. **Reuses that foundation verbatim** (react-i18next + i18next-icu + browser detector + key-parity/no-bare-strings CI + self-hosted OFL Noto via `@fontsource` + Gemini-draft→native-review). This is a **parallel** line: it adds locales, it does **not** re-architect i18n.
> **Honesty note:** each claim tagged `[verified]` (checked vs the working tree this session), `[researched]` (sourced, needs sign-off), or `[design]` (proposed). Not legal advice — the OFL-font carve-out is the owner's standing call (D7 in FRIENDLY).

---

## 0. TL;DR
Add **Thai, Indonesian, Danish, Spanish, Chinese (Simplified), Japanese, Korean** to the Studio SPA with the **same rigor as en/kn/hi**. The good news `[verified]`: the i18n foundation already scales — **zero changes to the negotiation contract or the catalog format**; adding a locale is "drop a reviewed `common.json` + (if non-Latin) bundle one OFL Noto font + keep CI key-parity green." The work splits cleanly into **Phase A** (es/da/id — *catalog-only, no fonts*), **Phase B** (Thai — *one small OFL font*), and **Phase C** (zh-Hans/ja/ko — *large OFL CJK fonts + lazy-load + an offline-bundle subsetting decision + the zh script-variant call*). **The single most important caveats:** (1) **native-speaker review per language** is the real cost and the public-launch gate (Gemini drafts are *inference-only*, never the source of truth — the standing guardrail); (2) **CJK fonts add tens of MB** to the otherwise-tiny air-gapped bundle; (3) **Spanish now has a 3-form plural** (`{one, many, other}`) — the one authoring wrinkle.

## 1. Problem & goal
**Why now.** en/kn/hi shipped and proved the foundation is locale-pluggable (a Kannada-default browser auto-renders Kannada; the switcher walks kn→hi→en). The owner wants to broaden reach beyond the India beachhead — **SE Asia (th, id), Europe (da, es), and East Asia (zh, ja, ko)**.
**Concrete outcome ("good"):** a user whose browser is in any of the 7 languages lands on the Studio (and the marketing pages too — FRIENDLY B3 landed [#70], with es/da/id site parity shipped [#87]; th/zh/ja/ko site parity still pending), and — without help — understands the product, runs the tool, and gets a reel, **entirely in their language**, with the fonts rendering crisply offline.
**Read to get current:** this doc §3–§4; [`FRIENDLY_AND_MULTILINGUAL.md`](archives/FRIENDLY_AND_MULTILINGUAL.md) (the foundation this rides on, esp. §4 design + §6.5 negotiation tests); `web/src/i18n/index.ts` (the detector/ICU/fallback config) + `web/src/main.tsx` (the `@fontsource` font-load pattern) + `web/src/i18n/hygiene.test.ts` (key-parity + no-bare-strings + offline guards).

## 2. Decisions locked (inherited from FRIENDLY unless noted)
| # | Decision | Source / date | Implication |

=== changelog tail (last 3 entries) ===
194:- `2026-06-22` — **remaining home content sections localized + responsive header smoothed ([#90](https://github.com/avidullu/khelsutra/pull/90)).** Wired the last hardcoded home-page sections — **`#diff`/`#sharper`/`#ask`/`#roadmap`/`#not`/`#faq`** (**65 new `site.{diff,sharper,ask,roadmap,not,faq}.*` keys**) — so the **home page body is now fully localized in all 6 languages except the `#join` access form** (A6, deferred). en byte-for-byte from the DOM; kn/hi/es/da/id via a per-locale **translate → 2-lens adversarial verify → revise** Workflow (20 agents); inline markup + brand/tokens preserved. The same PR **smoothed the responsive header** — the ~860–1080px dead zone where the 6 nav-links wrapped onto 3 lines, overlapped the brand, and clipped the CTA — by tightening the links and hiding them below 1080px (a clean brand + switcher + CTA row). Verified in-browser en/id @ 1280/1000/864/375px. **Site 84 + web 223 green.**
195:- `2026-06-22` — **▶ browser layout-regression CI guard + full-locale header fix ([#91](https://github.com/avidullu/khelsutra/pull/91)).** Closed the gap that let the header bug recur 3×: the suites run in **jsdom, which has no layout engine** (`getBoundingClientRect()` is all zeros), so overflow/wrapping/clipping was invisible to CI. New **`layout` CI job** → `npm run test:layout` drives a headless **Chromium** (`site/src/layout.browser.mjs`; Playwright = a site test-only devDep) over index/capture/own-model at widths **[1280,1100,1000,900,768,375] × all 6 locales**, asserting header **invariants** (no nav overflow / links never wrap / CTA not clipped / no page h-scroll). Assertions are **inequalities, not screenshots** → robust across OS/fonts, no baselines. It immediately caught that **#90's header fix was incomplete** — **es (+138px)** and **da (+47px)** still overflowed (their translated nav labels are far longer). Fixed with **measure-and-collapse**: the shim's new **`fitNav`** measures the header after applying a locale (+ on resize / `fonts.ready`) and adds `.nav-crowded` to collapse the nav when the links won't fit — **measured live, so it self-adapts to any future translation length**. en keeps its 6 links; es collapses cleanly. Proven to **fail on the pre-fix CSS, pass after**; green in CI's Ubuntu env. **Site 85 + web 223 + the new layout job green.**
196:- `2026-06-22` — **chapter-closeout doc sweep + stronger fitNav tests ([#92](https://github.com/avidullu/khelsutra/pull/92)).** Brought this tracker's changelog + §9 DoD current (logged #89/#90/#91); strengthened the `fitNav` shim unit test to deterministically cover both branches (collapse-on-overflow / reveal-on-fit + idempotency) by mocking geometry, since jsdom has no layout engine. Site 86 green.
197:- `2026-06-22` — **▶ ARCHIVED — Friendly & Multilingual chapter complete.** Engineering is done across both surfaces — 6 languages end-to-end, the home-page body fully localized (except the deferred `#join` form, A6), the recurring responsive-header bug fixed + **CI-guarded** (#91), and this tracker swept current (#92). Per **owner directive**, archived on **engineering-complete**: `git mv` → `docs/archives/`; inbound pointers (`NEXT_STEPS.md`, `docs/README.md`, `docs/MULTILINGUAL_EXPANSION.md`, the `web/src/i18n/` design-rationale comments) updated. The two **human gates** (native-speaker review of the AI-drafted catalogs + the owner font-glyph eyeball on a real kn/hi device) and the deferred **B7** (engine backend l10n) are **carried forward to `NEXT_STEPS.md`** as live owner items — not archival blockers.
